+import TCSlib.Complexity.CircuitComplexity.FormulaArith.CarrySave
+
+/-!
+# Formula arithmetic: carry-out and comparison with a constant
+
+For two little-endian formula numbers `U, W` (`FormulaArith.val`), the carry into bit position
+`b` of `U + W` is a formula of depth `2⌈log₂ b⌉ + 3` over the bits, by the generate/propagate
+expansion `carry_b = ⋁_{j < b} (U_j ∧ W_j ∧ ⋀_{j < k < b} (U_k ∨ W_k))` with balanced fan-in-2
+trees; its value is `Adder.lean`'s ripple carry `carryBit`.  The test `c ≤ val U + val W` for a
+constant `c` is the carry-out of `U + W + (2^p - c)` after one more carry-save step.  No
+construction here is a truth table over the bit length: the depth is logarithmic in it
+(r3 §H3.3).
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `BoolCircuit.FormulaArith.bitAt` — the bit sequence of a formula number under an assignment.
+* `BoolCircuit.FormulaArith.carryOut` — the carry into position `b` of `U + W`.
+* `BoolCircuit.FormulaArith.constBits` — the low `p` bits of a constant, as constant formulas.
+* `BoolCircuit.FormulaArith.geConst` — the comparison `c ≤ val U + val W`.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `BoolCircuit.FormulaArith.carryOut_eval`, `carryOut_eval_le`, `depth_carryOut_le`,
+  `maxFanin_carryOut_le` — F7: the value is `carryBit`, hence `2 ^ b ≤ val U + val W` for
+  `b`-bit numbers; depth `2⌈log₂ b⌉ + 3` over the bits; fan-in two (targets).
+* `BoolCircuit.FormulaArith.geConst_eval`, `depth_geConst_le`, `maxFanin_geConst_le` — F8:
+  the comparison with a constant, its depth and fan-in (targets).
+
+## References
+
+* [BV11] Z. Brakerski, V. Vaikuntanathan, *Efficient Fully Homomorphic Encryption from
+  (Standard) LWE*, FOCS 2011, pp. 97–106; full version, IACR ePrint 2011/344 (39 pages,
+  2 March 2012), §4.5, Lemma 4.5.  Context only: the declarations of this file are glue for
+  the comparison step of the modular interval sum (`ModSum.lean`) and carry no tag.
+-/
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+namespace BoolCircuit
+
+namespace FormulaArith
+
+variable {n : ℕ}
+
+/-! ### Bit sequences and the carry-out (F7) -/
+
+/-- The bit sequence of a little-endian formula number under the assignment `x`, padded with
+`false`: `bitAt x U j = (U.map (·.eval x)).getD j false`, the bit sequence whose
+`prefixVal` over `|U|` bits is `val x U` (`Adder.lean`'s `bitsVal_eq_prefixVal`).  Glue. -/
+def bitAt (x : Fin n → Bool) (U : List (TreeCircuit n)) (j : ℕ) : Bool :=
+  (U.map fun c => c.eval x).getD j false
+
+/-- The bit sequence of a formula number: `bitAt x U j = (U.map fun c => c.eval x).getD j false`.
+-/
+theorem bitAt_eq (x : Fin n → Bool) (U : List (TreeCircuit n)) (j : ℕ) :
+    bitAt x U j = (U.map fun c => c.eval x).getD j false := rfl
+
+/-- A bit padded by `const false` evaluates to the bit sequence:
+`(U.getD j (TreeCircuit.const false)).eval x = bitAt x U j`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem eval_getD_const (x : Fin n → Bool) (U : List (TreeCircuit n)) (j : ℕ) :
+    (U.getD j (TreeCircuit.const false)).eval x = bitAt x U j := by
+  have h := List.getD_map U (TreeCircuit.const false) (n := j) fun c : TreeCircuit n => c.eval x
+  rw [TreeCircuit.const_eval] at h
+  rw [bitAt_eq, h]
+
+/-- A formula number is the value of its bit sequence over any `m ≥ |U|` bits:
+`U.length ≤ m → val x U = prefixVal (bitAt x U) m`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem val_eq_prefixVal_bitAt (x : Fin n → Bool) {U : List (TreeCircuit n)} {m : ℕ}
+    (hm : U.length ≤ m) : val x U = prefixVal (bitAt x U) m := by
+  calc val x U = prefixVal (bitAt x U) U.length := by
+        rw [val_eq, bitsVal_eq_prefixVal, List.length_map]
+        exact prefixVal_congr fun j _ => (bitAt_eq x U j).symm
+    _ = prefixVal (bitAt x U) m :=
+        (prefixVal_eq_of_false hm fun j hj => by
+          rw [bitAt_eq, List.getD_eq_default _ _ (by rw [List.length_map]; exact hj)]).symm
+
+/-- The carry into bit position `b` of `U + W`, by generate/propagate: with `U_j, W_j` the bits
+padded by `const false`, `carryOut U W b = ⋁_{j < b} ⋀ [U_j ∧ W_j, U_{j+1} ∨ W_{j+1}, …,
+U_{b-1} ∨ W_{b-1}]` (bit `j` generates a carry and every higher bit below `b` transmits it),
+the outer `∨` over `j < b` and each inner `∧` as `TreeCircuit.balanced` fan-in-2 trees.  Its
+value is `carryBit` (`carryOut_eval`), its depth `2⌈log₂ b⌉ + 3` over the bits
+(`depth_carryOut_le`).  Glue. -/
+def carryOut (U W : List (TreeCircuit n)) (b : ℕ) : TreeCircuit n :=
+  TreeCircuit.balanced false ((List.range b).map fun j =>
+    TreeCircuit.balanced true
+      (TreeCircuit.and2 (U.getD j (TreeCircuit.const false)) (W.getD j (TreeCircuit.const false)) ::
+        (List.range (b - 1 - j)).map fun k =>
+          TreeCircuit.or2 (U.getD (j + 1 + k) (TreeCircuit.const false))
+            (W.getD (j + 1 + k) (TreeCircuit.const false))))
+
+/-- The carry-out formula written out: with `z = const false`,
+`carryOut U W b = balanced false ((range b).map fun j => balanced true
+(and2 (U.getD j z) (W.getD j z) :: (range (b - 1 - j)).map fun k =>
+or2 (U.getD (j + 1 + k) z) (W.getD (j + 1 + k) z)))`. -/
+theorem carryOut_eq (U W : List (TreeCircuit n)) (b : ℕ) :
+    carryOut U W b =
+      TreeCircuit.balanced false ((List.range b).map fun j =>
+        TreeCircuit.balanced true
+          (TreeCircuit.and2 (U.getD j (TreeCircuit.const false))
+              (W.getD j (TreeCircuit.const false)) ::
+            (List.range (b - 1 - j)).map fun k =>
+              TreeCircuit.or2 (U.getD (j + 1 + k) (TreeCircuit.const false))
+                (W.getD (j + 1 + k) (TreeCircuit.const false)))) := rfl
+
+/-- The ripple carry into position `b` is set exactly when some bit `j < b` generates a carry
+and every bit strictly between `j` and `b` transmits it:
+`carryBit A B b = true ↔ ∃ j < b, A j = true ∧ B j = true ∧
+∀ i, j < i → i < b → (A i || B i) = true`.  Glue.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Induction on `b` with `carryBit_zero`/`carryBit_succ`.  At `b + 1`, the carry
+is `(A_b ∧ B_b) ∨ ((A_b ⊕ B_b) ∧ carry_b)`, which equals `(A_b ∧ B_b) ∨ ((A_b ∨ B_b) ∧ carry_b)`
+(cases on the Booleans): the first disjunct is the witness `j = b`, the second extends a
+witness `j < b` of the induction hypothesis by the transmitting bit `b`. -/
+private theorem carryBit_eq_true_iff (A B : ℕ → Bool) (b : ℕ) :
+    carryBit A B b = true ↔
+      ∃ j < b, A j = true ∧ B j = true ∧ ∀ i, j < i → i < b → (A i || B i) = true := by
+  induction b with
+  | zero =>
+      rw [carryBit_zero]
+      exact ⟨fun h => absurd h Bool.false_ne_true, fun ⟨j, hj, _⟩ => absurd hj (Nat.not_lt_zero j)⟩
+  | succ b ih =>
+      rw [carryBit_succ]
+      constructor
+      · intro h
+        by_cases hg : A b = true ∧ B b = true
+        · exact ⟨b, Nat.lt_succ_self b, hg.1, hg.2, fun i hi hib => absurd hib (by omega)⟩
+        -- Step 1: no generation at `b`, so `b` transmits and there is a carry into `b`
+        have ht : (A b || B b) = true ∧ carryBit A B b = true := by
+          revert h hg
+          cases A b <;> cases B b <;> cases carryBit A B b <;> decide
+        obtain ⟨j, hj, hAj, hBj, hm⟩ := ih.1 ht.2
+        refine ⟨j, by omega, hAj, hBj, fun i hji hib => ?_⟩
+        rcases Nat.lt_succ_iff_lt_or_eq.1 hib with hib | rfl
+        · exact hm i hji hib
+        · exact ht.1
+      · rintro ⟨j, hj, hAj, hBj, hm⟩
+        rcases Nat.lt_succ_iff_lt_or_eq.1 hj with hj | rfl
+        -- Step 2: a witness below `b` gives a carry into `b`, which bit `b` transmits
+        · have hc := ih.2 ⟨j, hj, hAj, hBj, fun i h1 h2 => hm i h1 (by omega)⟩
+          have hor := hm b hj (Nat.lt_succ_self b)
+          rw [hc]
+          revert hor
+          cases A b <;> cases B b <;> decide
+        · simp only [hAj, hBj, Bool.and_self, Bool.true_or]
+
+/-- **F7, value.** The carry-out formula computes the ripple carry into position `b`:
+`(carryOut U W b).eval x = carryBit (bitAt x U) (bitAt x W) b` (`Adder.lean:377`).  Glue
+(r3 §H5.7, F7).  *Statement change:* r3's sketch writes `carryOut U W` with the position `b`
+free; the position is the explicit third argument.
+
+**Proof sketch.** Induction on `b` for the unrolled carry: `carryBit A B (i + 1) =
+(A_i ∧ B_i) ∨ ((A_i ⊕ B_i) ∧ carryBit A B i)` (`carryBit_zero`, `carryBit_succ`) equals
+`(A_i ∧ B_i) ∨ ((A_i ∨ B_i) ∧ carryBit A B i)` by cases, and unrolling gives
+`carryBit A B b = ∃ j < b, A_j ∧ B_j ∧ ∀ k, j < k < b → A_k ∨ B_k` (`carryBit_eq_true_iff`).
+On the formula side `carryOut_eq`, `balanced_eval`, `eval_node_false_iff`/`eval_node_true_iff`,
+`and2_eval`, `or2_eval`, and each padded bit evaluates to `bitAt x U j` (`eval_getD_const`:
+`bitAt_eq`, `const_eval` for the padding, `List.getD_map`); the inner index `k` of disjunct `j`
+is the bit `j + 1 + k`. -/
+theorem carryOut_eval (U W : List (TreeCircuit n)) (b : ℕ) (x : Fin n → Bool) :
+    (carryOut U W b).eval x = carryBit (bitAt x U) (bitAt x W) b := by
+  rw [Bool.eq_iff_iff, carryOut_eq, TreeCircuit.balanced_eval, TreeCircuit.eval_node_false_iff,
+    carryBit_eq_true_iff]
+  constructor
+  -- Step 1: a true disjunct `j` is a generate/propagate witness
+  · rintro ⟨c, hc, hce⟩
+    obtain ⟨j, hj, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hc
+    rw [List.mem_range] at hj
+    rw [TreeCircuit.balanced_eval, TreeCircuit.eval_node_true_iff] at hce
+    have h0 := hce _ List.mem_cons_self
+    rw [TreeCircuit.and2_eval, eval_getD_const, eval_getD_const, Bool.and_eq_true] at h0
+    refine ⟨j, hj, h0.1, h0.2, fun i hji hib => ?_⟩
+    have hji : j + 1 + (i - (j + 1)) = i := by omega
+    have hi := hce (TreeCircuit.or2 (U.getD i (TreeCircuit.const false))
+      (W.getD i (TreeCircuit.const false))) (List.mem_cons_of_mem _ (List.mem_map.2
+        ⟨i - (j + 1), List.mem_range.2 (by omega), by rw [hji]⟩))
+    rwa [TreeCircuit.or2_eval, eval_getD_const, eval_getD_const] at hi
+  -- Step 2: a witness `j` makes disjunct `j` true
+  · rintro ⟨j, hj, hA, hB, hm⟩
+    refine ⟨_, List.mem_map.2 ⟨j, List.mem_range.2 hj, rfl⟩, ?_⟩
+    rw [TreeCircuit.balanced_eval, TreeCircuit.eval_node_true_iff]
+    intro c hc
+    rcases List.mem_cons.1 hc with rfl | hc
+    · rw [TreeCircuit.and2_eval, eval_getD_const, eval_getD_const, hA, hB]
+      rfl
+    · obtain ⟨k, hk, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hc
+      rw [List.mem_range] at hk
+      rw [TreeCircuit.or2_eval, eval_getD_const, eval_getD_const]
+      exact hm _ (by omega) (by omega)
+
+/-- **F7, the threshold.** For numbers of at most `b` bits the carry into position `b` is the
+test `2 ^ b ≤ U + W`: `U.length ≤ b → W.length ≤ b →
+(carryOut U W b).eval x = decide (2 ^ b ≤ val x U + val x W)`.  Glue (r3 §H5.7, F7).
+
+**Proof sketch.** `carryOut_eval`, then `prefixVal_add` (`Adder.lean:397`) at `b`:
+`prefixVal A b + prefixVal B b = prefixVal (sumBit A B) b + 2 ^ b · carry`.  Here
+`prefixVal (bitAt x U) b = val x U` (`val_eq_prefixVal_bitAt`: `val_eq`, `bitsVal_eq_prefixVal`,
+`prefixVal_congr`, and `prefixVal_eq_of_false` to extend from `|U|` to `b` bits by `false`), and
+`prefixVal (sumBit A B) b < 2 ^ b` (`prefixVal_lt`), so the carry is `1` exactly when
+`2 ^ b ≤ val x U + val x W`. -/
+theorem carryOut_eval_le {U W : List (TreeCircuit n)} {b : ℕ} (hU : U.length ≤ b)
+    (hW : W.length ≤ b) (x : Fin n → Bool) :
+    (carryOut U W b).eval x = decide (2 ^ b ≤ val x U + val x W) := by
+  rw [carryOut_eval]
+  have hadd := prefixVal_add (bitAt x U) (bitAt x W) b
+  rw [← val_eq_prefixVal_bitAt x hU, ← val_eq_prefixVal_bitAt x hW] at hadd
+  have hlt := prefixVal_lt (sumBit (bitAt x U) (bitAt x W)) b
+  cases hc : carryBit (bitAt x U) (bitAt x W) b
+  · rw [hc, Bool.toNat_false, Nat.mul_zero, Nat.add_zero] at hadd
+    exact (decide_eq_false (by omega)).symm
+  · rw [hc, Bool.toNat_true, Nat.mul_one] at hadd
+    exact (decide_eq_true (by omega)).symm
+
+/-- **F7, depth.** The carry-out lies `2⌈log₂ b⌉ + 3` levels above the bits (counted from at
+least `1`, the depth of the padding constants):
+`(carryOut U W b).depth ≤ max 1 (TreeCircuit.maxDepth (U ++ W)) + 2 * Nat.clog 2 b + 3`.
+Glue (r3 §H5.7, F7); r3's draft "depth `2⌈log₂(b+1)⌉ + 2`" *(fix)* is settled by this
+construction at `2⌈log₂ b⌉ + 3` over the inputs.
+
+**Proof sketch.** With `D = max 1 (maxDepth (U ++ W))`: every padded bit has depth `≤ D`
+(`depth_getD_le`: `depth_le_maxDepth`, `depth_const`), so every `and2`/`or2` item has depth
+`≤ D + 1` (`depth_and2_le`, `depth_or2_le`, `maxDepth_le_of_forall`).  The inner list for
+`j < b` has `b - j ≤ b` items, so its `balanced true` tree has depth `≤ D + 1 + ⌈log₂ b⌉ + 1`
+(`depth_balanced_le`, `Nat.clog_mono_right`); the outer list has `b` items, so the outer
+`balanced false` tree adds `⌈log₂ b⌉ + 1` more (`depth_balanced_le`, `maxDepth_le_of_forall`). -/
+theorem depth_carryOut_le (U W : List (TreeCircuit n)) (b : ℕ) :
+    (carryOut U W b).depth ≤ max 1 (TreeCircuit.maxDepth (U ++ W)) + 2 * Nat.clog 2 b + 3 := by
+  -- Step 1: every padded bit lies within `D = max 1 (maxDepth (U ++ W))`
+  have hpad : ∀ V : List (TreeCircuit n), (∀ c ∈ V, c ∈ U ++ W) → ∀ i,
+      (V.getD i (TreeCircuit.const false)).depth ≤ max 1 (TreeCircuit.maxDepth (U ++ W)) :=
+    fun V hV => depth_getD_le
+      (fun c hc => (TreeCircuit.depth_le_maxDepth (hV c hc)).trans (le_max_right _ _))
+      (le_max_left _ _)
+  have hU := hpad U fun c hc => List.mem_append_left _ hc
+  have hW := hpad W fun c hc => List.mem_append_right _ hc
+  -- Step 2: the outer balanced tree over `b` inner trees
+  rw [carryOut_eq]
+  refine le_trans (TreeCircuit.depth_balanced_le false _) ?_
+  rw [List.length_map, List.length_range]
+  refine le_trans (Nat.add_le_add_right (Nat.add_le_add_right (TreeCircuit.maxDepth_le_of_forall
+    (d := max 1 (TreeCircuit.maxDepth (U ++ W)) + Nat.clog 2 b + 2) fun c hc => ?_) _) _) ?_
+  · -- Step 3: each inner balanced tree over at most `b` gates `and2`/`or2`
+    obtain ⟨j, hj, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hc
+    rw [List.mem_range] at hj
+    refine le_trans (TreeCircuit.depth_balanced_le true _) ?_
+    refine le_trans (Nat.add_le_add_right (Nat.add_le_add (TreeCircuit.maxDepth_le_of_forall
+      (d := max 1 (TreeCircuit.maxDepth (U ++ W)) + 1) fun c hc => ?_)
+      (Nat.clog_mono_right 2 (?_ : _ ≤ b))) _) ?_
+    · rcases List.mem_cons.1 hc with rfl | hc
+      · refine (TreeCircuit.depth_and2_le _ _).trans ?_
+        have := hU j; have := hW j
+        omega
+      · obtain ⟨k, -, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hc
+        refine (TreeCircuit.depth_or2_le _ _).trans ?_
+        have := hU (j + 1 + k); have := hW (j + 1 + k)
+        omega
+    · rw [List.length_cons, List.length_map, List.length_range]
+      omega
+    · omega
+  · omega
+
+/-- **F7, fan-in two.**
+`(carryOut U W b).maxFanin ≤ max 2 (TreeCircuit.maxFaninL (U ++ W))`.  Glue.
+
+**Proof sketch.** `carryOut_eq`; `maxFanin_balanced_le` for both trees, `maxFanin_and2_le`,
+`maxFanin_or2_le`, and each padded bit has fan-in `≤ maxFaninL (U ++ W)` (`maxFanin_getD_le`:
+`maxFanin_le_maxFaninL`, `maxFanin_const`); a bound on every member bounds each list's maximum
+(`maxFaninL_le_of_forall`, by list induction with `maxFaninL_cons`). -/
+theorem maxFanin_carryOut_le (U W : List (TreeCircuit n)) (b : ℕ) :
+    (carryOut U W b).maxFanin ≤ max 2 (TreeCircuit.maxFaninL (U ++ W)) := by
+  -- Step 1: every padded bit has fan-in at most `maxFaninL (U ++ W)`
+  have hpad : ∀ V : List (TreeCircuit n), (∀ c ∈ V, c ∈ U ++ W) → ∀ i,
+      (V.getD i (TreeCircuit.const false)).maxFanin ≤ TreeCircuit.maxFaninL (U ++ W) :=
+    fun V hV => maxFanin_getD_le fun c hc => TreeCircuit.maxFanin_le_maxFaninL (hV c hc)
+  have hU := hpad U fun c hc => List.mem_append_left _ hc
+  have hW := hpad W fun c hc => List.mem_append_right _ hc
+  -- Step 2: both balanced trees, then the `and2`/`or2` items
+  rw [carryOut_eq]
+  refine le_trans (TreeCircuit.maxFanin_balanced_le false _)
+    (max_le (le_max_left _ _) (TreeCircuit.maxFaninL_le_of_forall fun c hc => ?_))
+  obtain ⟨j, -, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hc
+  refine le_trans (TreeCircuit.maxFanin_balanced_le true _)
+    (max_le (le_max_left _ _) (TreeCircuit.maxFaninL_le_of_forall fun c hc => ?_))
+  rcases List.mem_cons.1 hc with rfl | hc
+  · refine (TreeCircuit.maxFanin_and2_le _ _).trans ?_
+    have := hU j; have := hW j
+    omega
+  · obtain ⟨k, -, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hc
+    refine (TreeCircuit.maxFanin_or2_le _ _).trans ?_
+    have := hU (j + 1 + k); have := hW (j + 1 + k)
+    omega
+
+/-! ### Comparison with a constant (F8) -/
+
+/-- The low `p` bits of the constant `c` as constant formulas, little-endian:
+`constBits p c = (range p).map fun j => const (c.testBit j)`, of value `c % 2 ^ p`.  Glue. -/
+def constBits (p c : ℕ) : List (TreeCircuit n) :=
+  (List.range p).map fun j => TreeCircuit.const (c.testBit j)
+
+/-- The constant's bits as formulas:
+`constBits p c = (List.range p).map fun j => TreeCircuit.const (c.testBit j)`. -/
+theorem constBits_eq (p c : ℕ) :
+    (constBits p c : List (TreeCircuit n)) =
+      (List.range p).map fun j => TreeCircuit.const (c.testBit j) := rfl
+
+/-- The comparison of `U + W` with the constant `c`: with `p = max |U| |W| + 1` (so that
+`val U + val W < 2 ^ p`), `geConst U W c` is `const true` for `c = 0`, `const false` for
+`2 ^ p < c`, and otherwise the carry-out at position `p` of the carry-save step of `U`, `W` and
+the `p`-bit constant `2 ^ p - c`:
+`carryOut (csaStep U W (constBits p (2^p - c))).1 (csaStep U W (constBits p (2^p - c))).2 p`,
+since `c ≤ U + W ↔ 2 ^ p ≤ U + W + (2 ^ p - c)`.  Glue (r3 §H3.4 item 3). -/
+def geConst (U W : List (TreeCircuit n)) (c : ℕ) : TreeCircuit n :=
+  if c = 0 then TreeCircuit.const true
+  else if 2 ^ (max U.length W.length + 1) < c then TreeCircuit.const false
+  else
+    carryOut
+      (csaStep U W (constBits (max U.length W.length + 1)
+        (2 ^ (max U.length W.length + 1) - c))).1
+      (csaStep U W (constBits (max U.length W.length + 1)
+        (2 ^ (max U.length W.length + 1) - c))).2
+      (max U.length W.length + 1)
+
+/-- The comparison formula written out: with `p = max U.length W.length + 1`,
+`geConst U W c = if c = 0 then const true else if 2 ^ p < c then const false else
+carryOut (csaStep U W (constBits p (2 ^ p - c))).1 (csaStep U W (constBits p (2 ^ p - c))).2 p`.
+-/
+theorem geConst_eq (U W : List (TreeCircuit n)) (c : ℕ) :
+    geConst U W c =
+      if c = 0 then TreeCircuit.const true
+      else if 2 ^ (max U.length W.length + 1) < c then TreeCircuit.const false
+      else
+        carryOut
+          (csaStep U W (constBits (max U.length W.length + 1)
+            (2 ^ (max U.length W.length + 1) - c))).1
+          (csaStep U W (constBits (max U.length W.length + 1)
+            (2 ^ (max U.length W.length + 1) - c))).2
+          (max U.length W.length + 1) := rfl
+
+/-- **F8, value.** The comparison formula tests `c ≤ U + W`, for all `U`, `W` and `c`:
+`(geConst U W c).eval x = decide (c ≤ val x U + val x W)`.  Glue (r3 §H5.7, F8).
+
+**Proof sketch.** `geConst_eq`, with `p = max |U| |W| + 1`, so `val x U, val x W < 2 ^ (p - 1)`
+(`val_eq`, `bitsVal_eq_prefixVal`, `prefixVal_lt`) and `val x U + val x W < 2 ^ p`.  For
+`c = 0` and for `2 ^ p < c` both sides are constant (`const_eval`).  Otherwise
+`T = 2 ^ p - c < 2 ^ p` and `val x (constBits p T) = T` (`val_eq_prefixVal_bitAt`,
+`constBits_eq`, `const_eval`, and `prefixVal c.testBit p = c % 2 ^ p` by `prefixVal_testBit`:
+`prefixVal_succ`, `Nat.mod_pow_succ`, `Nat.toNat_testBit`); `csaStep_val` gives
+`val S + val K = val U + val W + T`, with `|S| = p` and `|K| = p + 1` (`csaStep_eq`).  The top
+bit of the carry word `K` is `maj3` of the padding of `U`, `W` (both `const false`, as
+`|U|, |W| ≤ p - 1`) and a bit of `T`, so it is `false` (`csaStep_eq`, `maj3_eval`) and
+`val K = prefixVal (bitAt x K) p`; hence `carryOut_eval` and `prefixVal_add` at `p`, with
+`prefixVal_lt` for the sum bits, give the carry into position `p` as
+`decide (2 ^ p ≤ val S + val K)`, which is `decide (c ≤ val x U + val x W)`. -/
+theorem geConst_eval (U W : List (TreeCircuit n)) (c : ℕ) (x : Fin n → Bool) :
+    (geConst U W c).eval x = decide (c ≤ val x U + val x W) := by
+  obtain ⟨p, hp⟩ : ∃ p, max U.length W.length + 1 = p := ⟨_, rfl⟩
+  -- Step 1: `val x U + val x W < 2 ^ p`
+  have hpow : 2 ^ p = 2 ^ (p - 1) * 2 := by
+    rw [← pow_succ]
+    congr 1
+    omega
+  have hvU : val x U < 2 ^ (p - 1) := by
+    rw [val_eq_prefixVal_bitAt x (le_refl U.length)]
+    exact lt_of_lt_of_le (prefixVal_lt _ _) (Nat.pow_le_pow_right (by norm_num) (by omega))
+  have hvW : val x W < 2 ^ (p - 1) := by
+    rw [val_eq_prefixVal_bitAt x (le_refl W.length)]
+    exact lt_of_lt_of_le (prefixVal_lt _ _) (Nat.pow_le_pow_right (by norm_num) (by omega))
+  rw [geConst_eq, hp]
+  split_ifs with h0 h1
+  -- Step 2: the two constant cases
+  · rw [TreeCircuit.const_eval, h0]
+    exact (decide_eq_true (Nat.zero_le _)).symm
+  · rw [TreeCircuit.const_eval]
+    exact (decide_eq_false (by omega)).symm
+  -- Step 3: the constant `T = 2 ^ p - c` as `p` constant bits
+  obtain ⟨T, hT⟩ : ∃ T, 2 ^ p - c = T := ⟨_, rfl⟩
+  rw [hT]
+  have hV : (constBits p T : List (TreeCircuit n)).length = p := by
+    rw [constBits_eq, List.length_map, List.length_range]
+  have hvV : val x (constBits p T) = T := by
+    rw [val_eq_prefixVal_bitAt x hV.le, prefixVal_congr (g := T.testBit) fun j hj => ?_,
+      prefixVal_testBit, Nat.mod_eq_of_lt (by omega)]
+    rw [← eval_getD_const, constBits_eq,
+      List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ (by rw [List.length_map, List.length_range]; exact hj),
+      List.getElem_map, List.getElem_range, TreeCircuit.const_eval]
+  -- Step 4: one carry-save step; the sum word has `p` bits, the carry word `p + 1`
+  have hcsa := csaStep_val x U W (constBits p T)
+  have hS : (csaStep U W (constBits p T)).1.length = p := by
+    rw [csaStep_eq]
+    dsimp only
+    rw [List.length_map, List.length_range, hV]
+    omega
+  have hK : (csaStep U W (constBits p T)).2.length = p + 1 := by
+    rw [csaStep_eq]
+    dsimp only
+    rw [List.length_cons, List.length_map, List.length_range, hV]
+    omega
+  -- Step 5: the top carry bit is `maj3` of two padding bits and a bit of `T`, so `false`
+  have htop : bitAt x (csaStep U W (constBits p T)).2 p = false := by
+    rw [← eval_getD_const, csaStep_eq]
+    dsimp only
+    obtain ⟨q, rfl⟩ : ∃ q, p = q + 1 := ⟨p - 1, by omega⟩
+    rw [List.getD_cons_succ,
+      List.getD_eq_getElem _ _ (by rw [List.length_map, List.length_range, hV]; omega),
+      List.getElem_map, List.getElem_range, TreeCircuit.maj3_eval,
+      List.getD_eq_default U _ (by omega), List.getD_eq_default W _ (by omega),
+      TreeCircuit.const_eval]
+    cases ((constBits (q + 1) T : List (TreeCircuit n)).getD q (TreeCircuit.const false)).eval x
+      <;> rfl
+  -- Step 6: the carry into position `p` of `S + K` tests `2 ^ p ≤ val S + val K`
+  have hvS := val_eq_prefixVal_bitAt x hS.le
+  have hvK : val x (csaStep U W (constBits p T)).2 =
+      prefixVal (bitAt x (csaStep U W (constBits p T)).2) p := by
+    rw [val_eq_prefixVal_bitAt x hK.le, prefixVal_succ, htop, Bool.toNat_false, Nat.zero_mul,
+      Nat.add_zero]
+  rw [carryOut_eval]
+  have hadd := prefixVal_add (bitAt x (csaStep U W (constBits p T)).1)
+    (bitAt x (csaStep U W (constBits p T)).2) p
+  have hlt := prefixVal_lt (sumBit (bitAt x (csaStep U W (constBits p T)).1)
+    (bitAt x (csaStep U W (constBits p T)).2)) p
+  cases hc : carryBit (bitAt x (csaStep U W (constBits p T)).1)
+      (bitAt x (csaStep U W (constBits p T)).2) p
+  · rw [hc, Bool.toNat_false, Nat.mul_zero, Nat.add_zero] at hadd
+    exact (decide_eq_false (by omega)).symm
+  · rw [hc, Bool.toNat_true, Nat.mul_one] at hadd
+    exact (decide_eq_true (by omega)).symm
+
+/-- **F8, depth.** The comparison lies `2⌈log₂(max |U| |W| + 1)⌉ + 7` levels above the inputs
+(counted from at least `1`):
+`(geConst U W c).depth ≤ max 1 (TreeCircuit.maxDepth (U ++ W)) +
+2 * Nat.clog 2 (max U.length W.length + 1) + 7`, independent of `c`.  Glue (r3 §H5.7, F8;
+r3 names the lemma without a formula).
+
+**Proof sketch.** `geConst_eq`.  The constant cases have depth `1` (`depth_const`).  Otherwise,
+with `D = max 1 (maxDepth (U ++ W))`: the constant bits have depth `1` (`constBits_eq`,
+`depth_const`), so `maxDepth (U ++ W ++ constBits …) ≤ D` (`maxDepth_le_of_forall`),
+`depth_csaStep_le` bounds both words by `D + 4`, and `depth_carryOut_le` at
+`p = max |U| |W| + 1` adds `2⌈log₂ p⌉ + 3`. -/
+theorem depth_geConst_le (U W : List (TreeCircuit n)) (c : ℕ) :
+    (geConst U W c).depth ≤
+      max 1 (TreeCircuit.maxDepth (U ++ W)) + 2 * Nat.clog 2 (max U.length W.length + 1) + 7 := by
+  rw [geConst_eq]
+  generalize max U.length W.length + 1 = p
+  have hD : 1 ≤ max 1 (TreeCircuit.maxDepth (U ++ W)) := le_max_left _ _
+  split_ifs
+  · rw [TreeCircuit.depth_const]
+    omega
+  · rw [TreeCircuit.depth_const]
+    omega
+  -- Step 1: the inputs of the carry-save step, the constant bits included, lie within `D`
+  have hin : TreeCircuit.maxDepth (U ++ W ++ constBits p (2 ^ p - c)) ≤
+      max 1 (TreeCircuit.maxDepth (U ++ W)) := TreeCircuit.maxDepth_le_of_forall fun d hd => by
+    rcases List.mem_append.1 hd with hd | hd
+    · exact (TreeCircuit.depth_le_maxDepth hd).trans (le_max_right _ _)
+    · rw [constBits_eq] at hd
+      obtain ⟨j, -, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hd
+      rw [TreeCircuit.depth_const]
+      exact le_max_left _ _
+  -- Step 2: `+4` for the step, `+ 2⌈log₂ p⌉ + 3` for the carry-out
+  have hcsa := depth_csaStep_le U W (constBits p (2 ^ p - c))
+  refine le_trans (depth_carryOut_le _ _ _) ?_
+  omega
+
+/-- **F8, fan-in two.**
+`(geConst U W c).maxFanin ≤ max 2 (TreeCircuit.maxFaninL (U ++ W))`.  Glue.
+
+**Proof sketch.** `geConst_eq`; `maxFanin_const` for the constant cases, otherwise
+`maxFanin_carryOut_le` and `maxFaninL_csaStep_le` (the constant bits have fan-in `0`, so
+`maxFaninL (U ++ W ++ constBits …) ≤ maxFaninL (U ++ W)` by `maxFaninL_le_of_forall`). -/
+theorem maxFanin_geConst_le (U W : List (TreeCircuit n)) (c : ℕ) :
+    (geConst U W c).maxFanin ≤ max 2 (TreeCircuit.maxFaninL (U ++ W)) := by
+  rw [geConst_eq]
+  generalize max U.length W.length + 1 = p
+  split_ifs
+  · rw [TreeCircuit.maxFanin_const]
+    exact Nat.zero_le _
+  · rw [TreeCircuit.maxFanin_const]
+    exact Nat.zero_le _
+  have hin : TreeCircuit.maxFaninL (U ++ W ++ constBits p (2 ^ p - c)) ≤
+      TreeCircuit.maxFaninL (U ++ W) := TreeCircuit.maxFaninL_le_of_forall fun d hd => by
+    rcases List.mem_append.1 hd with hd | hd
+    · exact TreeCircuit.maxFanin_le_maxFaninL hd
+    · rw [constBits_eq] at hd
+      obtain ⟨j, -, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hd
+      rw [TreeCircuit.maxFanin_const]
+      exact Nat.zero_le _
+  have hcsa := maxFaninL_csaStep_le U W (constBits p (2 ^ p - c))
+  refine le_trans (maxFanin_carryOut_le _ _ _) ?_
+  omega
+
+end FormulaArith
+
+end BoolCircuit

+import Mathlib.Algebra.BigOperators.Fin
+import Mathlib.Algebra.BigOperators.Group.Finset.Defs
+import Mathlib.Algebra.Order.BigOperators.Group.Finset
+import TCSlib.Complexity.CircuitComplexity.FormulaArith.Compare
+
+/-!
+# Formula arithmetic: the modular interval sum
+
+For public constants `a : Fin M → ℕ` with `a i < q` and an interval `[lo, hi) ⊆ [0, q)`, the
+formula `FormulaArith.modSumInterval a q lo hi` on the bits `x : Fin M → Bool` tests
+`(∑ i, x_i · a_i) mod q ∈ [lo, hi)`.  This is the decryption formula's core
+([BV11, Lemma 4.5]; [BV14, Prop 3.4]): decryption is a binary subset sum of public constants
+followed by an interval test (r3 §H3.2–H3.3).  The construction (r3 §H3.4):
+1. masked constants `x_i · a_i`, each bit the literal `x_i` or the constant `false`;
+2. the carry-save reduction of the `M` summands to two numbers `U, W` with `U + W = S`
+   (`csaReduce`), trimmed to `⌈log₂ M⌉ + ⌈log₂ q⌉` bits (as `S < max M 1 · q`);
+3. for each `j < max M 1` the two-sided test `j q + lo ≤ S < j q + hi` by two comparisons
+   with constants (`geConst`), and
+4. the `∨` over `j` by a balanced fan-in-2 tree.
+
+Its depth is `O(log M + log log q)` with an explicit constant (`modSumDepth`) and no term
+linear in `log q`: no Shannon, DNF or truth-table step touches a `log q`-bit value.
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `BoolCircuit.FormulaArith.maskedConst` — the number `x_i · a` as a list of formulas.
+* `BoolCircuit.FormulaArith.modSumInterval` — the modular interval-sum formula.
+* `BoolCircuit.FormulaArith.modSumDepth` — its depth bound, in closed form over `csaLevels`.
+
+## Main results
+
+* `BoolCircuit.FormulaArith.modSumInterval_eval` — F9: the formula tests
+  `lo ≤ (∑ i, x_i a_i) % q < hi` (target).
+* `BoolCircuit.FormulaArith.depth_modSumInterval_le`, `modSumDepth_le`,
+  `maxFanin_modSumInterval_le` — F10: depth at most
+  `9⌈log₂ M⌉ + 2⌈log₂(⌈log₂ q⌉ + ⌈log₂ M⌉ + 3)⌉ + 21`, fan-in two (targets).
+
+## References
+
+* [BV11] Z. Brakerski, V. Vaikuntanathan, *Efficient Fully Homomorphic Encryption from
+  (Standard) LWE*, FOCS 2011, pp. 97–106; full version, IACR ePrint 2011/344 (39 pages,
+  2 March 2012), §4.5, Lemma 4.5 and its proof.
+* [BV14] Z. Brakerski, V. Vaikuntanathan, *Lattice-Based FHE as Secure as PKE*, ITCS 2014,
+  pp. 1–12; IACR ePrint 2013/541, §3.1, Proposition 3.4.
+-/
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+namespace BoolCircuit
+
+namespace FormulaArith
+
+variable {n : ℕ}
+
+/-- The masked constant `x_i · a` as a `p`-bit little-endian number: bit `j` is the literal
+`x_i` where `a` has a `1` and `const false` elsewhere,
+`maskedConst i a p = (range p).map fun j => if a.testBit j then lit ⟨i, true⟩ else const false`,
+of value `(x i).toNat * (a % 2 ^ p)`.  Glue (r3 §H3.4 item 1). -/
+def maskedConst (i : Fin n) (a p : ℕ) : List (TreeCircuit n) :=
+  (List.range p).map fun j =>
+    if a.testBit j then TreeCircuit.lit ⟨i, true⟩ else TreeCircuit.const false
+
+/-- The masked constant written out: `maskedConst i a p = (List.range p).map fun j =>
+if a.testBit j then TreeCircuit.lit ⟨i, true⟩ else TreeCircuit.const false`. -/
+theorem maskedConst_eq (i : Fin n) (a p : ℕ) :
+    maskedConst i a p =
+      (List.range p).map fun j =>
+        if a.testBit j then TreeCircuit.lit ⟨i, true⟩ else TreeCircuit.const false := rfl
+
+/-- The modular interval-sum formula on `M` bits `x`: with `B = ⌈log₂ q⌉` and
+`w = ⌈log₂ M⌉ + ⌈log₂ q⌉`, let `(U, W) = csaReduce [maskedConst i (a i) B | i < M]` (so
+`U + W = ∑ i, x_i a_i` when every `a i < q`), trimmed to `w` bits; then
+`modSumInterval a q lo hi = ⋁_{j < max M 1} (geConst U W (j q + lo) ∧ ¬ geConst U W (j q + hi))`,
+the `∨` a `balanced false` tree and `¬` the De Morgan `neg`.  It tests
+`lo ≤ (∑ i, x_i a_i) % q < hi` (`modSumInterval_eval`).  [BV11, Lemma 4.5, p029-b001;
+BV14, Prop 3.4, p011-b001]; deviations in `modSumInterval_eval`. -/
+def modSumInterval {M : ℕ} (a : Fin M → ℕ) (q lo hi : ℕ) : TreeCircuit M :=
+  let UW := csaReduce (List.ofFn fun i => maskedConst i (a i) (Nat.clog 2 q))
+  let U := UW.1.take (Nat.clog 2 M + Nat.clog 2 q)
+  let W := UW.2.take (Nat.clog 2 M + Nat.clog 2 q)
+  TreeCircuit.balanced false ((List.range (max M 1)).map fun j =>
+    TreeCircuit.and2 (geConst U W (j * q + lo)) (geConst U W (j * q + hi)).neg)
+
+/-- The modular interval-sum formula written out: with
+`UW = csaReduce (List.ofFn fun i => maskedConst i (a i) (Nat.clog 2 q))`,
+`U = UW.1.take w`, `W = UW.2.take w` and `w = Nat.clog 2 M + Nat.clog 2 q`,
+`modSumInterval a q lo hi = balanced false ((range (max M 1)).map fun j =>
+and2 (geConst U W (j * q + lo)) (geConst U W (j * q + hi)).neg)`. -/
+theorem modSumInterval_eq {M : ℕ} (a : Fin M → ℕ) (q lo hi : ℕ) :
+    modSumInterval a q lo hi =
+      TreeCircuit.balanced false ((List.range (max M 1)).map fun j =>
+        TreeCircuit.and2
+          (geConst
+            ((csaReduce (List.ofFn fun i => maskedConst i (a i) (Nat.clog 2 q))).1.take
+              (Nat.clog 2 M + Nat.clog 2 q))
+            ((csaReduce (List.ofFn fun i => maskedConst i (a i) (Nat.clog 2 q))).2.take
+              (Nat.clog 2 M + Nat.clog 2 q))
+            (j * q + lo))
+          (geConst
+            ((csaReduce (List.ofFn fun i => maskedConst i (a i) (Nat.clog 2 q))).1.take
+              (Nat.clog 2 M + Nat.clog 2 q))
+            ((csaReduce (List.ofFn fun i => maskedConst i (a i) (Nat.clog 2 q))).2.take
+              (Nat.clog 2 M + Nat.clog 2 q))
+            (j * q + hi)).neg) := rfl
+
+/-- A masked constant has the value `x_i · a` when `a < 2 ^ p`:
+`a < 2 ^ p → val x (maskedConst i a p) = (x i).toNat * a`.  Glue.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The bits evaluate to those of `c = if x i then a else 0` (`maskedConst_eq`,
+`eval_lit`, `Lit.eval_eq`, `const_eval`, `Nat.zero_testBit`); read the value through `val_eq`,
+`bitsVal_eq_prefixVal`, `prefixVal_congr` and `prefixVal_testBit`, and `a % 2 ^ p = a`. -/
+private theorem val_maskedConst (x : Fin n → Bool) (i : Fin n) {a p : ℕ} (ha : a < 2 ^ p) :
+    val x (maskedConst i a p) = (x i).toNat * a := by
+  -- Step 1: the bits are those of `c = if x i then a else 0`
+  have hbits : ((maskedConst i a p).map fun c => c.eval x) =
+      (List.range p).map (if x i = true then a else 0).testBit := by
+    rw [maskedConst_eq, List.map_map]
+    refine List.map_congr_left fun j _ => ?_
+    by_cases hj : a.testBit j
+    · rw [Function.comp_apply, if_pos hj, TreeCircuit.eval_lit, Lit.eval_eq]
+      by_cases hx : x i = true
+      · rw [if_pos rfl, if_pos hx, hx, hj]
+      · rw [if_pos rfl, if_neg hx, Nat.zero_testBit, Bool.eq_false_iff.2 hx]
+    · rw [Function.comp_apply, if_neg hj, TreeCircuit.const_eval]
+      by_cases hx : x i = true
+      · rw [if_pos hx, Bool.eq_false_iff.2 hj]
+      · rw [if_neg hx, Nat.zero_testBit]
+  -- Step 2: read the value through `prefixVal`
+  rw [val_eq, hbits, bitsVal_eq_prefixVal, List.length_map, List.length_range]
+  rw [prefixVal_congr (g := (if x i = true then a else 0).testBit) fun j hj => by
+    rw [List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getElem?_map, List.getElem?_range hj, Option.map_some,
+      Option.getD_some], prefixVal_testBit]
+  by_cases hx : x i = true
+  · rw [if_pos hx, hx, Bool.toNat_true, one_mul, Nat.mod_eq_of_lt ha]
+  · rw [if_neg hx, Bool.eq_false_iff.2 hx, Bool.toNat_false, zero_mul, Nat.zero_mod]
+
+/-- Bits above position `w` of a number below `2 ^ w` are zero:
+`w ≤ m → prefixVal f m < 2 ^ w → prefixVal f m = prefixVal f w`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem prefixVal_eq_of_lt_two_pow {f : ℕ → Bool} {w m : ℕ} (hwm : w ≤ m)
+    (h : prefixVal f m < 2 ^ w) : prefixVal f m = prefixVal f w := by
+  induction m, hwm using Nat.le_induction with
+  | base => rfl
+  | succ m hwm ih =>
+      have h2 : 2 ^ w ≤ 2 ^ m := Nat.pow_le_pow_right (by norm_num) hwm
+      rw [prefixVal_succ] at h ⊢
+      rcases Bool.eq_false_or_eq_true (f m) with hf | hf
+      · rw [hf, Bool.toNat_true, Nat.one_mul] at h
+        omega
+      · rw [hf, Bool.toNat_false, Nat.zero_mul, Nat.add_zero] at h ⊢
+        exact ih h
+
+/-- Trimming a formula number to `w` bits keeps its value when that value is below `2 ^ w`:
+`val x U < 2 ^ w → val x (U.take w) = val x U`.  Glue. -/
+private theorem val_take_of_lt (x : Fin n → Bool) {U : List (TreeCircuit n)} {w : ℕ}
+    (h : val x U < 2 ^ w) : val x (U.take w) = val x U := by
+  rcases le_total U.length w with hU | hU
+  · rw [List.take_of_length_le hU]
+  · rw [val_eq, bitsVal_eq_prefixVal] at h
+    have hU' : w ≤ (U.map fun c => c.eval x).length := by rwa [List.length_map]
+    rw [val_eq, val_eq, bitsVal_eq_prefixVal, bitsVal_eq_prefixVal, List.map_take,
+      List.length_take, Nat.min_eq_left hU']
+    refine (prefixVal_congr fun j hj => ?_).trans (prefixVal_eq_of_lt_two_pow hU' h).symm
+    rw [List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getD_eq_getElem?_getD, List.getElem?_take_of_lt hj]
+
+/-- **F9. The modular interval sum.** For constants `a i < q` and `lo ≤ hi ≤ q`, the formula
+tests whether the binary subset sum `∑ i, x_i · a_i` lies in `[lo, hi)` modulo `q`:
+`(modSumInterval a q lo hi).eval x =
+decide (lo ≤ (∑ i, (x i).toNat * a i) % q ∧ (∑ i, (x i).toNat * a i) % q < hi)`.
+[BV11, Lemma 4.5, p029-b001; BV14, Prop 3.4, p011-b001].  *Deviations:* BV11 computes the bit
+`(ŵ − ⟨v̂, ŝ⟩ mod p) mod 2` by subtracting all multiples of `p` and a selection tree; here the
+output is the interval test that decryption's `decode` is (r3 §H3.2–H3.3, O2), the summands are
+already the masked constants `x_i a_i` (BV11's `ŝ[i]^{(j)} · 2^j v̂[i]`), the summation is the
+carry-save tree (r3 §H3.4), and the reduction mod `q` is an `∨` of two-sided comparisons with
+the constants `j q + lo`, `j q + hi`.  The hypothesis `lo ≤ hi` is r3's; the probe finds the
+equation true without it (`hi ≤ q` and `a i < q` are needed).
+
+**Proof sketch.** (1) Each summand: `val x (maskedConst i (a i) B) = (x i).toNat * a i`
+(`val_maskedConst`: `maskedConst_eq`, `eval_lit`, `Lit.eval_eq`, `const_eval`, `val_eq`,
+`bitsVal_eq_prefixVal` and `prefixVal_testBit`, using `a i < q ≤ 2 ^ B`, `Nat.le_pow_clog`).  (2)
+`csaReduce_val` and `List.sum_ofFn`: `val U + val W = S := ∑ i, (x i).toNat * a i`.  (3) `S ≤ M (q -
+1) < 2 ^ w`, so both `val U, val W < 2 ^ w` and trimming to `w` bits keeps their values
+(`val_take_of_lt`).  (4) `balanced_eval`, `eval_node_false`, `and2_eval`, `neg_eval`,
+`geConst_eval`: the formula is `∃ j < max M 1, j q + lo ≤ S ∧ S < j q + hi`.  (5) Arithmetic: with
+`j = S / q < max M 1` this is `lo ≤ S % q < hi`, and no other `j` qualifies since `hi ≤ q`
+(`Nat.div_add_mod`). -/
+theorem modSumInterval_eval {M : ℕ} {a : Fin M → ℕ} {q lo hi : ℕ} (ha : ∀ i, a i < q)
+    (hlh : lo ≤ hi) (hhq : hi ≤ q) (x : Fin M → Bool) :
+    (modSumInterval a q lo hi).eval x =
+      decide (lo ≤ (∑ i, (x i).toNat * a i) % q ∧ (∑ i, (x i).toNat * a i) % q < hi) := by
+  set w := Nat.clog 2 M + Nat.clog 2 q with hw
+  set Us := List.ofFn fun i => maskedConst i (a i) (Nat.clog 2 q) with hUs
+  set S := ∑ i, (x i).toNat * a i with hS
+  -- Step 1: each summand is a masked constant of value `x_i a_i`; `S ≤ M (q - 1)`
+  have hq2 : ∀ i, a i < 2 ^ Nat.clog 2 q := fun i =>
+    (ha i).trans_le (Nat.le_pow_clog (by norm_num) q)
+  have hSle : S ≤ M * (q - 1) := by
+    have h := Finset.sum_le_card_nsmul Finset.univ (fun i => (x i).toNat * a i) (q - 1)
+      fun i _ => by
+        have := ha i
+        calc (x i).toNat * a i ≤ 1 * a i := Nat.mul_le_mul_right _ (Bool.toNat_le (x i))
+          _ ≤ q - 1 := by omega
+    rwa [Finset.card_univ, Fintype.card_fin, smul_eq_mul] at h
+  -- Step 2: the carry-save reduction keeps the sum
+  have hsum : val x (csaReduce Us).1 + val x (csaReduce Us).2 = S := by
+    rw [csaReduce_val, hUs, List.map_ofFn, List.sum_ofFn, hS]
+    exact Finset.sum_congr rfl fun i _ => val_maskedConst x i (hq2 i)
+  -- Step 3: `S < 2 ^ w`, so trimming to `w` bits keeps both values
+  have hS2 : S < 2 ^ w := by
+    rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos M with hM | hM
+    · rw [hM, Nat.zero_mul] at hSle
+      exact (Nat.le_zero.1 hSle).trans_lt (by positivity) |>.trans_le le_rfl
+    · have hq : 0 < q := (Nat.zero_le _).trans_lt (ha ⟨0, hM⟩)
+      calc S ≤ M * (q - 1) := hSle
+        _ < M * q := Nat.mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left (by omega) hM
+        _ ≤ 2 ^ Nat.clog 2 M * 2 ^ Nat.clog 2 q :=
+            Nat.mul_le_mul (Nat.le_pow_clog (by norm_num) M) (Nat.le_pow_clog (by norm_num) q)
+        _ = 2 ^ w := by rw [hw, pow_add]
+  have hV : val x ((csaReduce Us).1.take w) + val x ((csaReduce Us).2.take w) = S := by
+    rw [val_take_of_lt x (U := (csaReduce Us).1) (w := w) (by omega),
+      val_take_of_lt x (U := (csaReduce Us).2) (w := w) (by omega), hsum]
+  -- Step 4: the formula is `∃ j < max M 1, j q + lo ≤ S ∧ ¬ j q + hi ≤ S`
+  have hform : (modSumInterval a q lo hi).eval x = true ↔
+      ∃ j < max M 1, j * q + lo ≤ S ∧ ¬ j * q + hi ≤ S := by
+    rw [modSumInterval_eq, TreeCircuit.balanced_eval, TreeCircuit.eval_node_false_iff]
+    constructor
+    · rintro ⟨c, hc, hcx⟩
+      obtain ⟨j, hj, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hc
+      rw [TreeCircuit.and2_eval, Bool.and_eq_true, TreeCircuit.neg_eval, geConst_eval,
+        geConst_eval, ← hUs, ← hw, hV, Bool.not_eq_true', decide_eq_true_iff,
+        decide_eq_false_iff_not] at hcx
+      exact ⟨j, List.mem_range.1 hj, hcx⟩
+    · rintro ⟨j, hj, hjS⟩
+      refine ⟨_, List.mem_map.2 ⟨j, List.mem_range.2 hj, rfl⟩, ?_⟩
+      rw [TreeCircuit.and2_eval, Bool.and_eq_true, TreeCircuit.neg_eval, geConst_eval,
+        geConst_eval, ← hUs, ← hw, hV, Bool.not_eq_true', decide_eq_true_iff,
+        decide_eq_false_iff_not]
+      exact hjS
+  -- Step 5: arithmetic, `j = S / q`
+  rw [Bool.eq_iff_iff, hform, decide_eq_true_iff]
+  rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos q with hq | hq
+  · subst hq
+    have hhi : hi = 0 := Nat.le_zero.1 hhq
+    subst hhi
+    constructor
+    · rintro ⟨j, -, -, hj⟩
+      exact absurd (by rw [Nat.mul_zero, Nat.add_zero]; exact Nat.zero_le S) hj
+    · rintro ⟨-, h⟩
+      exact absurd h (Nat.not_lt_zero _)
+  · have hSq : S < max M 1 * q := by
+      rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos M with hM | hM
+      · rw [hM, Nat.zero_mul] at hSle
+        rw [Nat.le_zero.1 hSle, hM, max_eq_right (Nat.zero_le 1), Nat.one_mul]
+        exact hq
+      · rw [max_eq_left hM]
+        exact hSle.trans_lt (Nat.mul_lt_mul_of_pos_left (by omega) hM)
+    have hdm := Nat.div_add_mod S q
+    have hml := Nat.mod_lt S hq
+    constructor
+    · rintro ⟨j, -, hlo, hhi'⟩
+      have hj : S / q = j := Nat.div_eq_of_lt_le (by omega)
+        (by rw [Nat.add_mul, Nat.one_mul]; omega)
+      rw [hj, Nat.mul_comm] at hdm
+      constructor <;> omega
+    · rintro ⟨hlo, hhi'⟩
+      refine ⟨S / q, (Nat.div_lt_iff_lt_mul hq).2 hSq, ?_, ?_⟩
+      · rw [Nat.mul_comm]; omega
+      · rw [Nat.mul_comm]; omega
+
+/-- The depth bound of the modular interval-sum formula on `M` summands modulo `q`:
+`modSumDepth M q = 4 * csaLevels M + Nat.clog 2 M + 2 * Nat.clog 2 (Nat.clog 2 M + Nat.clog 2 q + 1)
++ 10` (carry-save tree `1 + 4 csaLevels M`, comparison `+ 2⌈log₂(w + 1)⌉ + 7` with
+`w = ⌈log₂ M⌉ + ⌈log₂ q⌉`, the `∧` `+1`, the `∨` over `max M 1` terms `+ ⌈log₂ M⌉ + 1`).
+Glue. -/
+def modSumDepth (M q : ℕ) : ℕ :=
+  4 * csaLevels M + Nat.clog 2 M + 2 * Nat.clog 2 (Nat.clog 2 M + Nat.clog 2 q + 1) + 10
+
+/-- The depth bound written out: `modSumDepth M q = 4 * csaLevels M + Nat.clog 2 M +
+2 * Nat.clog 2 (Nat.clog 2 M + Nat.clog 2 q + 1) + 10`. -/
+theorem modSumDepth_eq (M q : ℕ) :
+    modSumDepth M q =
+      4 * csaLevels M + Nat.clog 2 M + 2 * Nat.clog 2 (Nat.clog 2 M + Nat.clog 2 q + 1) + 10 :=
+  rfl
+
+/-- **F10, depth.** The modular interval-sum formula has depth at most `modSumDepth M q`, for
+all constants and bounds: `(modSumInterval a q lo hi).depth ≤ modSumDepth M q`.
+[BV11, Lemma 4.5, p029-b001: "fan-in 2 gates and O(log k + log log p) depth"; BV14, Prop 3.4,
+p011-b001]; *deviation:* an explicit constant instead of `O(·)`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** `modSumInterval_eq`.  The masked bits have depth `≤ 1` (`maskedConst_eq`,
+`depth_lit`, `depth_const`), so `depth_csaReduce_le` bounds `U ++ W` by `1 + 4 csaLevels M` (the
+summand list has length `M`, `List.length_ofFn`), and trimming does not increase it
+(`List.mem_of_mem_take`, `depth_le_maxDepth`, `maxDepth_le_of_forall`).  As `|U|, |W| ≤ w`,
+`depth_geConst_le` gives each comparison `≤ 4 csaLevels M + 8 + 2⌈log₂(w + 1)⌉`
+(`Nat.clog_mono_right`), `depth_neg` keeps it, `depth_and2_le` adds `1`, and `depth_balanced_le`
+over `max M 1` terms adds `⌈log₂ (max M 1)⌉ + 1 = ⌈log₂ M⌉ + 1`; the sum is `modSumDepth_eq`. -/
+theorem depth_modSumInterval_le {M : ℕ} (a : Fin M → ℕ) (q lo hi : ℕ) :
+    (modSumInterval a q lo hi).depth ≤ modSumDepth M q := by
+  set w := Nat.clog 2 M + Nat.clog 2 q with hw
+  set Us := List.ofFn fun i => maskedConst i (a i) (Nat.clog 2 q) with hUs
+  set U := (csaReduce Us).1.take w with hU
+  set W := (csaReduce Us).2.take w with hW
+  -- Step 1: the masked bits have depth at most `1`, so the reduction is `≤ 1 + 4 csaLevels M`
+  have hbits : TreeCircuit.maxDepth Us.flatten ≤ 1 := by
+    refine TreeCircuit.maxDepth_le_of_forall fun c hc => ?_
+    obtain ⟨V, hV, hcV⟩ := List.mem_flatten.1 hc
+    obtain ⟨i, rfl⟩ := List.mem_ofFn.1 hV
+    rw [maskedConst_eq] at hcV
+    obtain ⟨j, -, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hcV
+    split
+    · rw [TreeCircuit.depth_lit]; exact Nat.zero_le 1
+    · rw [TreeCircuit.depth_const]
+  have hred : TreeCircuit.maxDepth (U ++ W) ≤ 1 + 4 * csaLevels M := by
+    have h := depth_csaReduce_le Us
+    rw [hUs, List.length_ofFn, ← hUs, max_eq_left hbits] at h
+    refine TreeCircuit.maxDepth_le_of_forall fun c hc => ?_
+    refine le_trans (TreeCircuit.depth_le_maxDepth ?_) h
+    rcases List.mem_append.1 hc with hc | hc
+    · exact List.mem_append_left _ (List.mem_of_mem_take hc)
+    · exact List.mem_append_right _ (List.mem_of_mem_take hc)
+  -- Step 2: each comparison has depth `≤ 4 csaLevels M + 2⌈log₂ (w + 1)⌉ + 8`
+  have hlen : max U.length W.length + 1 ≤ w + 1 :=
+    Nat.add_le_add_right (max_le (List.length_take_le _ _) (List.length_take_le _ _)) 1
+  have hcmp : ∀ c, (geConst U W c).depth ≤
+      4 * csaLevels M + 2 * Nat.clog 2 (w + 1) + 8 := fun c => by
+    have h1 := depth_geConst_le U W c
+    have h2 := Nat.clog_mono_right 2 hlen
+    have h3 : max 1 (TreeCircuit.maxDepth (U ++ W)) ≤ 1 + 4 * csaLevels M :=
+      max_le (Nat.le_add_right 1 _) hred
+    omega
+  -- Step 3: the `∧`, then the balanced `∨` over `max M 1` terms
+  rw [modSumInterval_eq, ← hUs, ← hw, ← hU, ← hW]
+  refine (TreeCircuit.depth_balanced_le false _).trans ?_
+  have hterms : TreeCircuit.maxDepth ((List.range (max M 1)).map fun j =>
+      TreeCircuit.and2 (geConst U W (j * q + lo)) (geConst U W (j * q + hi)).neg) ≤
+      4 * csaLevels M + 2 * Nat.clog 2 (w + 1) + 9 := by
+    refine TreeCircuit.maxDepth_le_of_forall fun c hc => ?_
+    obtain ⟨j, -, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hc
+    refine (TreeCircuit.depth_and2_le _ _).trans ?_
+    rw [TreeCircuit.depth_neg]
+    exact Nat.add_le_add_right (max_le (hcmp _) (hcmp _)) 1
+  have hclog : Nat.clog 2 (max M 1) = Nat.clog 2 M := by
+    rcases Nat.eq_zero_or_pos M with hM | hM
+    · rw [hM, max_eq_right (Nat.zero_le 1), Nat.clog_one_right, Nat.clog_zero_right]
+    · rw [max_eq_left hM]
+  rw [List.length_map, List.length_range, hclog, modSumDepth_eq, ← hw]
+  omega
+
+/-- **F10, closed form.** The depth bound is `O(log M + log log q)` with no term linear in
+`log q`: `modSumDepth M q ≤
+9 * Nat.clog 2 M + 2 * Nat.clog 2 (Nat.clog 2 q + Nat.clog 2 M + 3) + 21`.
+[BV11, Lemma 4.5, p029-b001; BV14, Prop 3.4, p011-b001]; r3's draft closed form *(fix)*, kept:
+the construction meets it with room (it gives `9⌈log₂ M⌉ + 2⌈log₂(⌈log₂ q⌉ + ⌈log₂ M⌉ + 1)⌉
++ 10`); checked numerically in probe P10.
+
+**Proof sketch.** `modSumDepth_eq`; `csaLevels_le` gives `4 csaLevels M ≤ 8⌈log₂ M⌉`;
+`Nat.clog_mono_right` gives
+`⌈log₂(⌈log₂ M⌉ + ⌈log₂ q⌉ + 1)⌉ ≤ ⌈log₂(⌈log₂ q⌉ + ⌈log₂ M⌉ + 3)⌉`; then `omega`. -/
+theorem modSumDepth_le (M q : ℕ) :
+    modSumDepth M q ≤ 9 * Nat.clog 2 M + 2 * Nat.clog 2 (Nat.clog 2 q + Nat.clog 2 M + 3) + 21 := by
+  rw [modSumDepth_eq]
+  have h1 := csaLevels_le M
+  have h2 : Nat.clog 2 (Nat.clog 2 M + Nat.clog 2 q + 1) ≤
+      Nat.clog 2 (Nat.clog 2 q + Nat.clog 2 M + 3) := Nat.clog_mono_right 2 (by omega)
+  omega
+
+/-- **F10, fan-in two.** The modular interval-sum formula has fan-in at most two:
+`(modSumInterval a q lo hi).maxFanin ≤ 2`.  [BV11, Lemma 4.5, p029-b001: "fan-in 2 gates"].
+
+**Proof sketch.** `modSumInterval_eq`; the masked bits have fan-in `0` (`maxFanin_lit`,
+`maxFanin_const`), so `maxFaninL_csaReduce_le` gives `≤ 2` for `U ++ W` and for its
+trimming; then `maxFanin_geConst_le`, `maxFanin_neg`, `maxFanin_and2_le` and
+`maxFanin_balanced_le`. -/
+theorem maxFanin_modSumInterval_le {M : ℕ} (a : Fin M → ℕ) (q lo hi : ℕ) :
+    (modSumInterval a q lo hi).maxFanin ≤ 2 := by
+  set w := Nat.clog 2 M + Nat.clog 2 q with hw
+  set Us := List.ofFn fun i => maskedConst i (a i) (Nat.clog 2 q) with hUs
+  set U := (csaReduce Us).1.take w
+  set W := (csaReduce Us).2.take w
+  -- the masked bits have fan-in `0`, so the reduction and its trimming have fan-in `≤ 2`
+  have hbits : TreeCircuit.maxFaninL Us.flatten ≤ 0 := by
+    refine TreeCircuit.maxFaninL_le_of_forall fun c hc => ?_
+    obtain ⟨V, hV, hcV⟩ := List.mem_flatten.1 hc
+    obtain ⟨i, rfl⟩ := List.mem_ofFn.1 hV
+    rw [maskedConst_eq] at hcV
+    obtain ⟨j, -, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hcV
+    split
+    · rw [TreeCircuit.maxFanin_lit]
+    · rw [TreeCircuit.maxFanin_const]
+  have hred : TreeCircuit.maxFaninL (U ++ W) ≤ 2 := by
+    have h := maxFaninL_csaReduce_le Us
+    rw [Nat.le_zero.1 hbits, max_eq_left (Nat.zero_le 2)] at h
+    refine TreeCircuit.maxFaninL_le_of_forall fun c hc => ?_
+    refine le_trans (TreeCircuit.maxFanin_le_maxFaninL ?_) h
+    rcases List.mem_append.1 hc with hc | hc
+    · exact List.mem_append_left _ (List.mem_of_mem_take hc)
+    · exact List.mem_append_right _ (List.mem_of_mem_take hc)
+  have hcmp : ∀ c, (geConst U W c).maxFanin ≤ 2 := fun c =>
+    (maxFanin_geConst_le U W c).trans (max_le le_rfl hred)
+  rw [modSumInterval_eq, ← hUs, ← hw]
+  refine (TreeCircuit.maxFanin_balanced_le false _).trans (max_le le_rfl ?_)
+  refine TreeCircuit.maxFaninL_le_of_forall fun c hc => ?_
+  obtain ⟨j, -, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.1 hc
+  refine (TreeCircuit.maxFanin_and2_le _ _).trans (max_le le_rfl (max_le (hcmp _) ?_))
+  rw [TreeCircuit.maxFanin_neg]
+  exact hcmp _
+
+end FormulaArith
+
+end BoolCircuit

+/-
+Copyright (c) 2026 TCSlib contributors. All rights reserved.
+Released under Apache 2.0 license as described in the file LICENSE.
+Authors: TCSlib contributors

+import Mathlib.Algebra.Order.BigOperators.Group.List
+import TCSlib.Complexity.CircuitComplexity.DAGTransform
+
+/-!
+# NAND form of `DAGCircuit`s
+
+A *NAND gate* of `BoolCircuit.DAGCircuit` is the model's own `¬` gate read with at most two
+arguments: `DAGGate.eval` evaluates a `¬` gate as the **negated conjunction** of its arguments
+(`DAGGate.eval_eq`), so `⟨.not, [a, b]⟩` is `NAND(a, b)`, `⟨.not, [a]⟩` is `¬a = NAND(a)` and
+`⟨.not, []⟩` is the constant `false`, the negation of the empty conjunction.  A circuit is in
+*NAND form* when every gate is a NAND gate (`BoolCircuit.DAGCircuit.IsNANDForm`).
+
+The conversion `BoolCircuit.DAGCircuit.toNANDForm` takes every circuit whose gates read at most
+two vertices to NAND form.  It is an instance of the gate-rewriting host
+`BoolCircuit.DAGCircuit.rewrite` (`DAGTransform.lean`), with the gadget
+`BoolCircuit.nandGadget`, built in the style of `BoolCircuit.deMorganGadget` (`DAGFanin.lean`):
+
+| old gate | NAND gadget | gates | depth added |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| `∧[a, b]` | `t := NAND[a, b]`, `NAND[t]` | 2 | 2 |
+| `∨[a, b]` | `NAND[a]`, `NAND[b]`, `NAND[¬a, ¬b]` | 3 | 2 |
+| `∧[a]`, `∨[a]` | `NAND[a]`, `NAND[that]` | 2 | 2 |
+| `∧[]` (true) | `f := NAND[]`, `NAND[f]` | 2 | 2 (absolute) |
+| `∨[]` (false) | `NAND[]` | 1 | 1 (absolute) |
+| `¬[…]` | copied | 1 | 1 |
+
+Every gadget costs at most `fan-in + 2` gates and at most two levels, so the pass gives the
+semantics, depth at most `2 · depth`, and at most `n + 4 · #gates` vertices.
+
+**Disclosures.**
+* NAND-form circuits are **not** `BoolCircuit.DAGCircuit.IsWellFormed`, which demands `¬` gates of
+  fan-in exactly one; lemmas assuming well-formedness (`toTree_eval`, `deMorgan_*`,
+  `binarize_*`) do not apply to them.
+* The names avoid `NAnd`: `BoolCircuit.NAndCircuit` and `BoolCircuit.TreeCircuit.toNAnd`
+  (`Basic.lean`) are the AND-rooted alternating normal form of the LMN development, not NAND
+  gates.
+* No ingested source states that `{NAND}` is a complete basis or gives a conversion; the
+  conversion is glue, motivated by [BV14, Def 2.13, p008-b002] and [BV14, Thm 3.5, p014-b001]
+  ("every Boolean NAND circuit").
+
+## Main definitions
+
+* `BoolCircuit.DAGGate.IsNAND` — a `¬` gate reading at most two vertices.
+* `BoolCircuit.DAGCircuit.IsNANDForm` — every gate is a NAND gate.
+* `BoolCircuit.nandGadget` — the NAND gadget of the table above.
+* `BoolCircuit.DAGCircuit.toNANDForm` — `C.rewrite (nandGadget n) nandGadget_correct hC`.
+
+## Main results
+
+All are statement-phase targets (glue):
+* `BoolCircuit.DAGGate.eval_of_isNAND` (N1) — a NAND gate is the negated conjunction.
+* `BoolCircuit.nandGadget_correct` (N2) — the gadget meets `GadgetCorrect` with depth factor 2.
+* `BoolCircuit.DAGCircuit.toNANDForm_eval`, `toNANDForm_isNANDForm`, `toNANDForm_depth_le`,
+  `toNANDForm_size_le` (N3–N6) — semantics, form, depth `≤ 2 · depth`, size `≤ n + 4 · #gates`.
+
+While N2 is admitted, `sorryAx` reaches `toNANDForm` (whose body passes `nandGadget_correct` to
+`rewrite`) and its consumers through N2 alone.
+
+## References
+
+* [BV14] Z. Brakerski, V. Vaikuntanathan, *Lattice-based FHE as secure as PKE*, ITCS 2014;
+  ePrint 2013/541.
+-/
+
+set_option maxHeartbeats 0
+set_option relaxedAutoImplicit false
+set_option autoImplicit false
+
+namespace BoolCircuit
+
+variable {n : ℕ}
+
+/-! ## NAND gates and NAND form -/
+
+/-- A *NAND gate*: a `¬` gate (negated conjunction, `DAGGate.eval_eq`) reading at most two
+vertices, `g.IsNAND ↔ g.kind = .not ∧ g.args.length ≤ 2`.  Fan-in 1 is negation and fan-in 0
+the constant `false`.  Not `BoolCircuit.NAndCircuit` (`Basic.lean`, an AND-rooted normal form).
+Glue; motivated by [BV14, Def 2.13, p008-b002; Thm 3.5, p014-b001]. -/
+def DAGGate.IsNAND (g : DAGGate) : Prop := g.kind = .not ∧ g.args.length ≤ 2
+
+/-- The NAND-gate predicate unfolded: `g.IsNAND ↔ g.kind = .not ∧ g.args.length ≤ 2`. -/
+theorem DAGGate.isNAND_iff (g : DAGGate) : g.IsNAND ↔ g.kind = .not ∧ g.args.length ≤ 2 :=
+  Iff.rfl
+
+instance DAGGate.decidableIsNAND : DecidablePred DAGGate.IsNAND := fun g =>
+  inferInstanceAs (Decidable (g.kind = .not ∧ g.args.length ≤ 2))
+
+/-- A circuit is in *NAND form* when every gate is a NAND gate:
+`C.IsNANDForm ↔ ∀ g ∈ C.gates, g.IsNAND`.  Such circuits are not `IsWellFormed`, which demands
+fan-in-1 `¬` gates (disclosed).  Glue; motivated by [BV14, Def 2.13, p008-b002]. -/
+def DAGCircuit.IsNANDForm (C : DAGCircuit n) : Prop := ∀ g ∈ C.gates, g.IsNAND
+
+/-- NAND form unfolded: `C.IsNANDForm ↔ ∀ g ∈ C.gates, g.IsNAND`. -/
+theorem DAGCircuit.isNANDForm_iff (C : DAGCircuit n) :
+    C.IsNANDForm ↔ ∀ g ∈ C.gates, g.IsNAND :=
+  Iff.rfl
+
+instance DAGCircuit.decidableIsNANDForm (C : DAGCircuit n) : Decidable C.IsNANDForm :=
+  inferInstanceAs (Decidable (∀ g ∈ C.gates, g.IsNAND))
+
+/-- **N1. NAND semantics.** A NAND gate evaluates to the negated conjunction of the values it
+reads: `g.IsNAND → g.eval vals = !(g.args.all fun a => vals.getD a false)`.  Glue (a reader of
+`DAGGate.eval` on NAND gates).
+
+**Proof sketch.** Rewrite with `DAGGate.eval_eq` (`DAGCircuit.lean`); the kind is `.not` by the
+first component of `g.isNAND_iff`, so the match takes its `.not` branch. -/
+theorem DAGGate.eval_of_isNAND {g : DAGGate} (hg : g.IsNAND) (vals : List Bool) :
+    g.eval vals = !(g.args.all fun a => vals.getD a false) := by
+  rw [DAGGate.eval_eq, ((DAGGate.isNAND_iff g).mp hg).1]
+
+/-! ## The NAND gadget -/
+
+/-- The NAND gadget, compiling the gate `g` after the gates `gs` (vertex `n + gs.length` is the
+first new gate); it returns the new gate list and the vertex computing `g` (the table of the
+module docstring):
+* `¬[…]`: copied, `(gs ++ [g], n + |gs|)`;
+* `∧[]`: `f := NAND[]`, `NAND[f]`; `∨[]`: `NAND[]`;
+* `∧[a]`, `∨[a]`: `NAND[a]`, `NAND[that]`;
+* `∧[a, b]`: `t := NAND[a, b]`, `NAND[t]`; `∨[a, b]`: `NAND[a]`, `NAND[b]`, `NAND[¬a, ¬b]`;
+* an `∧`/`∨` gate of fan-in above two lies outside the gadget's domain (`nandGadget_correct`
+  assumes fan-in `≤ 2`) and is copied.
+Computable; glue, in the style of `deMorganGadget` (`DAGFanin.lean`). -/
+def nandGadget (n : ℕ) (g : DAGGate) (gs : List DAGGate) : List DAGGate × ℕ :=
+  match g.kind, g.args with
+  | .not, _ => (gs ++ [g], n + gs.length)
+  | .and, [] => (gs ++ [⟨.not, []⟩, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩], n + gs.length + 1)
+  | .or, [] => (gs ++ [⟨.not, []⟩], n + gs.length)
+  | .and, [a] => (gs ++ [⟨.not, [a]⟩, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩], n + gs.length + 1)
+  | .or, [a] => (gs ++ [⟨.not, [a]⟩, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩], n + gs.length + 1)
+  | .and, [a, b] => (gs ++ [⟨.not, [a, b]⟩, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩], n + gs.length + 1)
+  | .or, [a, b] =>
+    (gs ++ [⟨.not, [a]⟩, ⟨.not, [b]⟩, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length, n + gs.length + 1]⟩],
+      n + gs.length + 2)
+  | .and, _ :: _ :: _ :: _ => (gs ++ [g], n + gs.length)
+  | .or, _ :: _ :: _ :: _ => (gs ++ [g], n + gs.length)
+
+/-- The NAND gadget copies a `¬` gate:
+`nandGadget n ⟨.not, args⟩ gs = (gs ++ [⟨.not, args⟩], n + gs.length)`. -/
+theorem nandGadget_not (args : List ℕ) (gs : List DAGGate) :
+    nandGadget n ⟨.not, args⟩ gs = (gs ++ [⟨.not, args⟩], n + gs.length) := by
+  cases args with
+  | nil => rfl
+  | cons a l => rfl
+
+/-- The NAND gadget on the constant `∧[]` (true):
+`nandGadget n ⟨.and, []⟩ gs =
+(gs ++ [⟨.not, []⟩, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩], n + gs.length + 1)`. -/
+theorem nandGadget_and_nil (gs : List DAGGate) :
+    nandGadget n ⟨.and, []⟩ gs =
+      (gs ++ [⟨.not, []⟩, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩], n + gs.length + 1) := rfl
+
+/-- The NAND gadget on the constant `∨[]` (false):
+`nandGadget n ⟨.or, []⟩ gs = (gs ++ [⟨.not, []⟩], n + gs.length)`. -/
+theorem nandGadget_or_nil (gs : List DAGGate) :
+    nandGadget n ⟨.or, []⟩ gs = (gs ++ [⟨.not, []⟩], n + gs.length) := rfl
+
+/-- The NAND gadget on a fan-in-1 `∧` gate (double negation):
+`nandGadget n ⟨.and, [a]⟩ gs =
+(gs ++ [⟨.not, [a]⟩, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩], n + gs.length + 1)`. -/
+theorem nandGadget_and_singleton (a : ℕ) (gs : List DAGGate) :
+    nandGadget n ⟨.and, [a]⟩ gs =
+      (gs ++ [⟨.not, [a]⟩, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩], n + gs.length + 1) := rfl
+
+/-- The NAND gadget on a fan-in-1 `∨` gate (double negation):
+`nandGadget n ⟨.or, [a]⟩ gs =
+(gs ++ [⟨.not, [a]⟩, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩], n + gs.length + 1)`. -/
+theorem nandGadget_or_singleton (a : ℕ) (gs : List DAGGate) :
+    nandGadget n ⟨.or, [a]⟩ gs =
+      (gs ++ [⟨.not, [a]⟩, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩], n + gs.length + 1) := rfl
+
+/-- The NAND gadget on a fan-in-2 `∧` gate:
+`nandGadget n ⟨.and, [a, b]⟩ gs =
+(gs ++ [⟨.not, [a, b]⟩, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩], n + gs.length + 1)`. -/
+theorem nandGadget_and_pair (a b : ℕ) (gs : List DAGGate) :
+    nandGadget n ⟨.and, [a, b]⟩ gs =
+      (gs ++ [⟨.not, [a, b]⟩, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩], n + gs.length + 1) := rfl
+
+/-- The NAND gadget on a fan-in-2 `∨` gate (De Morgan):
+`nandGadget n ⟨.or, [a, b]⟩ gs = (gs ++ [⟨.not, [a]⟩, ⟨.not, [b]⟩,
+⟨.not, [n + gs.length, n + gs.length + 1]⟩], n + gs.length + 2)`. -/
+theorem nandGadget_or_pair (a b : ℕ) (gs : List DAGGate) :
+    nandGadget n ⟨.or, [a, b]⟩ gs =
+      (gs ++ [⟨.not, [a]⟩, ⟨.not, [b]⟩, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length, n + gs.length + 1]⟩],
+        n + gs.length + 2) := rfl
+
+/-- Outside its domain the NAND gadget copies the gate: for an `∧` or `∨` gate with at least
+three arguments, `nandGadget n ⟨k, a :: b :: c :: l⟩ gs = (gs ++ [⟨k, a :: b :: c :: l⟩],
+n + gs.length)` (`k ≠ .not`). -/
+theorem nandGadget_of_three_le {k : GateKind} (hk : k ≠ .not) (a b c : ℕ) (l : List ℕ)
+    (gs : List DAGGate) :
+    nandGadget n ⟨k, a :: b :: c :: l⟩ gs = (gs ++ [⟨k, a :: b :: c :: l⟩], n + gs.length) := by
+  cases k with
+  | and => rfl
+  | or => rfl
+  | not => exact absurd rfl hk
+
+/-! ## Correctness of the NAND gadget -/
+
+/-- Glue: over the depth run of `gs`, a gate has one level more than its deepest argument,
+measured by `vertexDepth`:
+`g.depth (runWith DAGGate.depth gs (List.replicate n 0)) =
+1 + (g.args.map (vertexDepth n gs)).foldr max 0`, by `DAGGate.depth_eq` and the reader
+`vertexDepth_eq` (`TreeDAG.lean`) on each argument. -/
+private theorem depth_runWith_eq {g : DAGGate} {gs : List DAGGate} :
+    g.depth (runWith DAGGate.depth gs (List.replicate n 0)) =
+      1 + (g.args.map (vertexDepth n gs)).foldr max 0 := by
+  rw [DAGGate.depth_eq, funext (vertexDepth_eq n gs)]
+
+/-- Glue: a one-gate gadget.  Emitting a single NAND gate `g₁` that reads the arguments of `g`
+and computes `g` meets `GadgetSpec` with depth factor `2`:
+`GadgetSpec n 2 (g.args.length + 2) DAGGate.IsNAND g gs (gs ++ [g₁], n + gs.length)`.  It
+covers the copied `¬` gate and `∨[] ↦ NAND[]`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The new vertex is the last one, so its value and depth are `g₁`'s over the
+run of `gs` (`vertexValue_last`, `vertexDepth_last`); `depth_runWith_eq` gives one level over
+the deepest argument. -/
+private theorem gadgetSpec_single {g g₁ : DAGGate} {gs : List DAGGate} (hgs : GatesAcyclic n gs)
+    (hargs : ∀ a ∈ g.args, a < n + gs.length) (hg₁ : g₁.IsNAND) (hga : g₁.args = g.args)
+    (hval : ∀ vals, g₁.eval vals = g.eval vals) :
+    GadgetSpec n 2 (g.args.length + 2) DAGGate.IsNAND g gs (gs ++ [g₁], n + gs.length) := by
+  have hargs₁ : ∀ a ∈ g₁.args, a < n + gs.length := hga ▸ hargs
+  refine ⟨⟨[g₁], rfl⟩, hgs.snoc hargs₁, ?_, fun x => ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
+  · simp only [List.length_append, List.length_singleton]; omega
+  · exact (vertexValue_last gs g₁ x).trans (hval _)
+  · simp only [List.length_append, List.length_singleton]; omega
+  · show vertexDepth n (gs ++ [g₁]) (n + gs.length) ≤ _
+    rw [vertexDepth_last, depth_runWith_eq, hga]
+    omega
+  · intro h hh
+    simp only [List.drop_left, List.mem_singleton] at hh
+    exact hh ▸ hg₁
+
+/-- Glue: a two-gate gadget.  Emitting a NAND gate `g₁` that reads the arguments of `g` and
+computes `¬g`, then its negation `NAND[g₁]`, meets `GadgetSpec` with depth factor `2`:
+`GadgetSpec n 2 (g.args.length + 2) DAGGate.IsNAND g gs
+(gs ++ [g₁, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩], n + gs.length + 1)`.  It covers `∧[]`, `∧[a]`, `∨[a]`
+and `∧[a, b]`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** The output vertex is the last one: its value is `¬` of vertex
+`n + |gs|`, which is `g₁`'s value over the run of `gs` (`vertexValue_last`,
+`runWith_getD_last`), hence `g`'s by `hval`.  Its depth is one more than `g₁`'s, which is one
+more than the deepest argument (`depth_runWith_eq`). -/
+private theorem gadgetSpec_double {g g₁ : DAGGate} {gs : List DAGGate} (hgs : GatesAcyclic n gs)
+    (hargs : ∀ a ∈ g.args, a < n + gs.length) (hg₁ : g₁.IsNAND) (hga : g₁.args = g.args)
+    (hval : ∀ vals, (!g₁.eval vals) = g.eval vals) :
+    GadgetSpec n 2 (g.args.length + 2) DAGGate.IsNAND g gs
+      (gs ++ [g₁, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩], n + gs.length + 1) := by
+  have hargs₁ : ∀ a ∈ g₁.args, a < n + gs.length := hga ▸ hargs
+  have e : gs ++ [g₁, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩] = gs ++ [g₁] ++ [⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩] := by
+    rw [List.append_assoc]; rfl
+  have hl₁ : (gs ++ [g₁]).length = gs.length + 1 := by
+    rw [List.length_append, List.length_singleton]
+  have hm : n + gs.length + 1 = n + (gs ++ [g₁]).length := by rw [hl₁]; omega
+  have h₂ : (⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩ : DAGGate).IsNAND :=
+    (DAGGate.isNAND_iff _).mpr ⟨rfl, by simp⟩
+  refine ⟨⟨[g₁, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩], rfl⟩, ?_, ?_, fun x => ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
+  · rw [e]
+    refine (hgs.snoc hargs₁).snoc fun a ha => ?_
+    rw [List.mem_singleton.mp ha, hl₁]
+    omega
+  · simp only [List.length_append, List.length_cons, List.length_nil]; omega
+  · -- value: the output negates vertex `n + |gs|`, which computes `¬g`
+    have hlast := runWith_getD_last DAGGate.eval gs g₁ (List.ofFn x) false
+    rw [List.length_ofFn] at hlast
+    show vertexValue (gs ++ [g₁, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩]) x (n + gs.length + 1) = _
+    rw [e, hm, vertexValue_last, DAGGate.eval_of_isNAND h₂, List.all_cons, List.all_nil,
+      Bool.and_true, hlast, hval]
+  · simp only [List.length_append, List.length_cons, List.length_nil]; omega
+  · -- depth: one level over `g₁`, which is one level over the deepest argument
+    have hlast := runWith_getD_last DAGGate.depth gs g₁ (List.replicate n 0) 0
+    rw [List.length_replicate, depth_runWith_eq, hga] at hlast
+    show vertexDepth n (gs ++ [g₁, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length]⟩]) (n + gs.length + 1) ≤ _
+    rw [e, hm, vertexDepth_last, DAGGate.depth_eq, List.map_cons, List.map_nil, List.foldr_cons,
+      List.foldr_nil, hlast]
+    omega
+  · intro h hh
+    simp only [List.drop_left, List.mem_cons, List.not_mem_nil, or_false] at hh
+    rcases hh with rfl | rfl
+    · exact hg₁
+    · exact h₂
+
+/-- Glue: the three-gate gadget of `∨[a, b]`, `NAND[a]`, `NAND[b]`, `NAND[¬a, ¬b]`, meets
+`GadgetSpec` with depth factor `2`:
+`GadgetSpec n 2 4 DAGGate.IsNAND ⟨.or, [a, b]⟩ gs (nandGadget n ⟨.or, [a, b]⟩ gs)`, written
+out by `nandGadget_or_pair`.
+
+**Proof sketch.** De Morgan: the output is `¬(¬a ∧ ¬b) = a ∨ b`.  The two negations sit at
+vertices `n + |gs|` and `n + |gs| + 1` and read only old vertices, whose values and depths
+the later gates do not change (`runWith_append`, `runWith_getD_of_lt`); each new vertex is
+evaluated by `runWith_getD_last` and `DAGGate.eval_of_isNAND`, and its depth by
+`depth_runWith_eq`.  The output is two levels above the deeper of `a`, `b`. -/
+private theorem gadgetSpec_orPair {a b : ℕ} {gs : List DAGGate} (hgs : GatesAcyclic n gs)
+    (hargs : ∀ v ∈ (⟨.or, [a, b]⟩ : DAGGate).args, v < n + gs.length) :
+    GadgetSpec n 2 ((⟨.or, [a, b]⟩ : DAGGate).args.length + 2) DAGGate.IsNAND ⟨.or, [a, b]⟩ gs
+      (gs ++ [⟨.not, [a]⟩, ⟨.not, [b]⟩, ⟨.not, [n + gs.length, n + gs.length + 1]⟩],
+        n + gs.length + 2) := by
+  have ha : a < n + gs.length := hargs a (by simp)
+  have hb : b < n + gs.length := hargs b (by simp)
+  have hN : ∀ l : List ℕ, l.length ≤ 2 → (⟨.not, l⟩ : DAGGate).IsNAND := fun l hl =>
+    (DAGGate.isNAND_iff _).mpr ⟨rfl, hl⟩
+  -- the three new gates, and the gate lists after one and two of them
+  set g₁ : DAGGate := ⟨.not, [a]⟩ with hg₁
+  set g₂ : DAGGate := ⟨.not, [b]⟩ with hg₂
+  set g₃ : DAGGate := ⟨.not, [n + gs.length, n + gs.length + 1]⟩ with hg₃
+  have e : gs ++ [g₁, g₂, g₃] = gs ++ [g₁] ++ [g₂] ++ [g₃] := by
+    simp only [List.append_assoc]; rfl
+  have hl₁ : (gs ++ [g₁]).length = gs.length + 1 := by
+    rw [List.length_append, List.length_singleton]
+  have hl₂ : (gs ++ [g₁] ++ [g₂]).length = gs.length + 2 := by
+    rw [List.length_append, hl₁, List.length_singleton]
+  have hm : n + gs.length + 2 = n + (gs ++ [g₁] ++ [g₂]).length := by rw [hl₂]; omega
+  refine ⟨⟨[g₁, g₂, g₃], rfl⟩, ?_, ?_, fun x => ?_, ?_, ?_, ?_⟩
+  · rw [e]
+    refine ((hgs.snoc fun v hv => ?_).snoc fun v hv => ?_).snoc fun v hv => ?_
+    · rw [List.mem_singleton.mp hv]; exact ha
+    · rw [List.mem_singleton.mp hv, hl₁]; omega
+    · simp only [hg₃, List.mem_cons, List.not_mem_nil, or_false] at hv
+      rw [hl₂]; omega
+  · simp only [List.length_append, List.length_cons, List.length_nil]; omega
+  · -- value: `¬(¬a ∧ ¬b) = a ∨ b`
+    set V := runWith DAGGate.eval gs (List.ofFn x)
+    set V₁ := runWith DAGGate.eval (gs ++ [g₁]) (List.ofFn x) with hV₁
+    have h₁ : V₁.getD (n + gs.length) false = g₁.eval V := by
+      have := runWith_getD_last DAGGate.eval gs g₁ (List.ofFn x) false
+      rwa [List.length_ofFn] at this
+    have h₂ : (runWith DAGGate.eval (gs ++ [g₁] ++ [g₂]) (List.ofFn x)).getD
+        (n + gs.length + 1) false = g₂.eval V₁ := by
+      have := runWith_getD_last DAGGate.eval (gs ++ [g₁]) g₂ (List.ofFn x) false
+      rwa [List.length_ofFn, hl₁, ← Nat.add_assoc] at this
+    have h₁₂ : (runWith DAGGate.eval (gs ++ [g₁] ++ [g₂]) (List.ofFn x)).getD
+        (n + gs.length) false = V₁.getD (n + gs.length) false := by
+      rw [runWith_append, runWith_getD_of_lt]
+      rw [length_runWith, List.length_ofFn, hl₁]; omega
+    have hb₁ : V₁.getD b false = V.getD b false := by
+      rw [hV₁, runWith_append, runWith_getD_of_lt]
+      rw [length_runWith, List.length_ofFn]; exact hb
+    show vertexValue (gs ++ [g₁, g₂, g₃]) x (n + gs.length + 2) = _
+    rw [e, hm, vertexValue_last, DAGGate.eval_of_isNAND (hN [n + gs.length, n + gs.length + 1]
+      (by simp)), List.all_cons, List.all_cons, List.all_nil, Bool.and_true, h₁₂, h₁, h₂, hg₁, hg₂,
+      DAGGate.eval_of_isNAND (hN [a] (by simp)), DAGGate.eval_of_isNAND (hN [b] (by simp))]
+    simp only [List.all_cons, List.all_nil, Bool.and_true]
+    rw [hb₁, DAGGate.eval_eq]
+    simp only [List.any_cons, List.any_nil, Bool.or_false]
+    cases V.getD a false <;> cases V.getD b false <;> rfl
+  · simp only [List.length_append, List.length_cons, List.length_nil]; omega
+  · -- depth: two levels over the deeper of `a` and `b`
+    have hd₁ : (runWith DAGGate.depth (gs ++ [g₁]) (List.replicate n 0)).getD (n + gs.length) 0
+        = 1 + max (vertexDepth n gs a) 0 := by
+      have := runWith_getD_last DAGGate.depth gs g₁ (List.replicate n 0) 0
+      rw [List.length_replicate, depth_runWith_eq] at this
+      rw [this, hg₁, List.map_cons, List.map_nil, List.foldr_cons, List.foldr_nil]
+    have hd₂ : (runWith DAGGate.depth (gs ++ [g₁] ++ [g₂]) (List.replicate n 0)).getD
+        (n + gs.length + 1) 0 = 1 + max (vertexDepth n gs b) 0 := by
+      have := runWith_getD_last DAGGate.depth (gs ++ [g₁]) g₂ (List.replicate n 0) 0
+      rw [List.length_replicate, hl₁, ← Nat.add_assoc, depth_runWith_eq, hg₂, List.map_cons,
+        List.map_nil, List.foldr_cons, List.foldr_nil, vertexDepth_append gs [g₁] hb] at this
+      exact this
+    have hd₁₂ : (runWith DAGGate.depth (gs ++ [g₁] ++ [g₂]) (List.replicate n 0)).getD
+        (n + gs.length) 0 =
+          (runWith DAGGate.depth (gs ++ [g₁]) (List.replicate n 0)).getD (n + gs.length) 0 := by
+      rw [runWith_append, runWith_getD_of_lt]
+      rw [length_runWith, List.length_replicate, hl₁]; omega
+    show vertexDepth n (gs ++ [g₁, g₂, g₃]) (n + gs.length + 2) ≤ _
+    rw [e, hm, vertexDepth_last, DAGGate.depth_eq, hg₃, List.map_cons, List.map_cons,
+      List.map_nil, List.foldr_cons, List.foldr_cons, List.foldr_nil, hd₁₂, hd₁, hd₂]
+    simp only [List.map_cons, List.map_nil, List.foldr_cons, List.foldr_nil]
+    omega
+  · intro h hh
+    simp only [List.drop_left, List.mem_cons, List.not_mem_nil, or_false] at hh
+    rcases hh with rfl | rfl | rfl
+    · exact hN _ (by simp)
+    · exact hN _ (by simp)
+    · exact hN _ (by simp)
+
+/-- **N2. The NAND gadget is correct.** In the sense of `GadgetCorrect` (`DAGTransform.lean`),
+with depth factor `2`, domain "fan-in at most two" and output predicate `DAGGate.IsNAND`:
+`GadgetCorrect n 2 (fun _ l => l ≤ 2) DAGGate.IsNAND (nandGadget n)`.  That is, for every gate
+`g` of fan-in at most two whose arguments are existing vertices of an acyclic gate list `gs`,
+`nandGadget n g gs` only appends to `gs`, stays acyclic, returns a vertex below the new length
+that computes `g`, adds at most `g.args.length + 2` gates and at most `2` levels over `g`'s
+deepest argument, and every appended gate is a NAND gate.  Glue (the table of the module
+docstring).
+
+**Proof sketch.** As `deMorganGadget_correct` (`DAGFanin.lean`): case on the kind and on the
+argument list (`nandGadget_not`, `nandGadget_and_nil`, …, the case readers above), and in each
+case check the seven fields of `GadgetSpec`.  Extension and length are immediate (`ext` is the
+emitted suffix; 1, 2 or 3 gates, at most `fan-in + 2`).  Acyclicity is `GatesAcyclic.snoc`
+once per emitted gate (each reads only `g`'s arguments, below `n + |gs|`, or the gates just
+emitted).  Values: `vertexValue_last` and `vertexValue_append` reduce each new vertex to a gate
+evaluation, `DAGGate.eval_of_isNAND` (N1) and `DAGGate.eval_eq` give `!(a && b)`, `!!a = a`,
+`!(!a && !b) = a || b`, `!true = false`, `!false = true` (`Bool` equation lemmas).  Depths:
+`vertexDepth_last`, `vertexDepth_append` and `DAGGate.depth_eq`: one level for a copied `¬` gate
+or `NAND[]`, two for the others.  New gates are `.not` gates of fan-in at most two by
+`DAGGate.isNAND_iff`.  The fill needs a reader for `GadgetCorrect`, which has none at the issue
+base (the owner-side `gadgetCorrect_iff`, plan section r3 §H2). -/
+theorem nandGadget_correct :
+    GadgetCorrect n 2 (fun _ l => l ≤ 2) DAGGate.IsNAND (nandGadget n) := by
+  rw [gadgetCorrect_iff]
+  intro g gs hgs hargs hl
+  obtain ⟨k, args⟩ := g
+  have hl' : args.length ≤ 2 := hl
+  have hN : ∀ l : List ℕ, l.length ≤ 2 → (⟨.not, l⟩ : DAGGate).IsNAND := fun l hl =>
+    (DAGGate.isNAND_iff _).mpr ⟨rfl, hl⟩
+  cases k with
+  | not =>
+    -- a `¬` gate is copied
+    rw [nandGadget_not]
+    exact gadgetSpec_single hgs hargs (hN args hl') rfl fun _ => rfl
+  | and =>
+    rcases args with _ | ⟨a, _ | ⟨b, _ | ⟨c, l⟩⟩⟩
+    · -- `∧[]`: `NAND[]`, then its negation
+      rw [nandGadget_and_nil]
+      refine gadgetSpec_double hgs hargs (hN [] (by simp)) rfl fun vals => ?_
+      rw [DAGGate.eval_eq, DAGGate.eval_eq]
+      simp only [List.all_nil, Bool.not_not]
+    · -- `∧[a]`: double negation
+      rw [nandGadget_and_singleton]
+      refine gadgetSpec_double hgs hargs (hN [a] (by simp)) rfl fun vals => ?_
+      rw [DAGGate.eval_eq, DAGGate.eval_eq]
+      simp only [List.all_cons, List.all_nil, Bool.and_true, Bool.not_not]
+    · -- `∧[a, b]`: `NAND[a, b]`, then its negation
+      rw [nandGadget_and_pair]
+      refine gadgetSpec_double hgs hargs (hN [a, b] (by simp)) rfl fun vals => ?_
+      rw [DAGGate.eval_eq, DAGGate.eval_eq]
+      simp only [List.all_cons, List.all_nil, Bool.and_true, Bool.not_not]
+    · simp only [List.length_cons] at hl'; omega
+  | or =>
+    rcases args with _ | ⟨a, _ | ⟨b, _ | ⟨c, l⟩⟩⟩
+    · -- `∨[]`: `NAND[]` is the constant `false`
+      rw [nandGadget_or_nil]
+      refine gadgetSpec_single hgs hargs (hN [] (by simp)) rfl fun vals => ?_
+      rw [DAGGate.eval_eq, DAGGate.eval_eq]
+      simp only [List.all_nil, List.any_nil, Bool.not_true]
+    · -- `∨[a]`: double negation
+      rw [nandGadget_or_singleton]
+      refine gadgetSpec_double hgs hargs (hN [a] (by simp)) rfl fun vals => ?_
+      rw [DAGGate.eval_eq, DAGGate.eval_eq]
+      simp only [List.all_cons, List.all_nil, List.any_cons, List.any_nil, Bool.and_true,
+        Bool.or_false, Bool.not_not]
+    · -- `∨[a, b]`: `NAND[¬a, ¬b]` (De Morgan), three gates
+      rw [nandGadget_or_pair]
+      exact gadgetSpec_orPair hgs hargs
+    · simp only [List.length_cons] at hl'; omega
+
+/-! ## The conversion -/
+
+/-- **The NAND form of a circuit** whose gates read at most two vertices: the gate-rewriting host
+applied to the NAND gadget, `C.toNANDForm hC = C.rewrite (nandGadget n) nandGadget_correct hC`.
+The domain is `∀ g ∈ C.gates, g.args.length ≤ 2` (not `IsFaninTwo`: a `¬` gate of fan-in two is
+already a NAND gate and is copied).  While N2 is admitted, `sorryAx` reaches this definition
+through `nandGadget_correct` only.  Glue; motivated by [BV14, Def 2.13, p008-b002;
+Thm 3.5, p014-b001]. -/
+def DAGCircuit.toNANDForm (C : DAGCircuit n) (hC : ∀ g ∈ C.gates, g.args.length ≤ 2) :
+    DAGCircuit n :=
+  C.rewrite (nandGadget n) nandGadget_correct hC
+
+/-- The NAND form unfolded:
+`C.toNANDForm hC = C.rewrite (nandGadget n) nandGadget_correct hC`. -/
+theorem DAGCircuit.toNANDForm_eq (C : DAGCircuit n) (hC : ∀ g ∈ C.gates, g.args.length ≤ 2) :
+    C.toNANDForm hC = C.rewrite (nandGadget n) nandGadget_correct hC := rfl
+
+section ToNANDForm
+
+variable (C : DAGCircuit n) (hC : ∀ g ∈ C.gates, g.args.length ≤ 2)
+
+/-- **N3. Semantics.** The NAND form computes the same function:
+`(C.toNANDForm hC).eval x = C.eval x`.  Glue.
+
+**Proof sketch.** `toNANDForm_eq`, then `DAGCircuit.rewrite_eval` (`DAGTransform.lean`) with the
+gadget's correctness N2. -/
+theorem DAGCircuit.toNANDForm_eval (x : Fin n → Bool) : (C.toNANDForm hC).eval x = C.eval x := by
+  rw [DAGCircuit.toNANDForm_eq]
+  exact DAGCircuit.rewrite_eval C (nandGadget n) nandGadget_correct hC x
+
+/-- **N4. Form.** The conversion is in NAND form: `(C.toNANDForm hC).IsNANDForm`.  Glue.
+
+**Proof sketch.** `isNANDForm_iff` and `toNANDForm_eq`, then `DAGCircuit.rewrite_new_gates`
+(`DAGTransform.lean`): every gate of a rewritten circuit satisfies the gadget's output
+predicate, here `DAGGate.IsNAND`. -/
+theorem DAGCircuit.toNANDForm_isNANDForm : (C.toNANDForm hC).IsNANDForm := by
+  rw [DAGCircuit.isNANDForm_iff, DAGCircuit.toNANDForm_eq]
+  exact DAGCircuit.rewrite_new_gates C (nandGadget n) nandGadget_correct hC
+
+/-- **N5. Depth.** The conversion at most doubles the depth:
+`(C.toNANDForm hC).depth ≤ 2 * C.depth`.  Glue.
+
+**Proof sketch.** `toNANDForm_eq`, then `DAGCircuit.rewrite_depth_le` (`DAGTransform.lean`)
+with the depth factor `c = 2` of N2. -/
+theorem DAGCircuit.toNANDForm_depth_le : (C.toNANDForm hC).depth ≤ 2 * C.depth := by
+  rw [DAGCircuit.toNANDForm_eq]
+  exact DAGCircuit.rewrite_depth_le C (nandGadget n) nandGadget_correct hC
+
+/-- **N6. Size.** The conversion has at most `n + 4 · #gates` vertices:
+`(C.toNANDForm hC).size ≤ n + 4 * C.gates.length`.  This is the generic `rewrite` bound
+(`fan-in + 2 ≤ 4` gates per old gate), not the tight `3 · #gates` (disclosed).  Glue.
+
+**Proof sketch.** `DAGCircuit.size_eq` on both sides and `toNANDForm_eq`; then
+`DAGCircuit.rewrite_length_le` (`DAGTransform.lean`) bounds the new gate count by
+`∑_{g ∈ C.gates} (g.args.length + 2)`, and each summand is at most `4` by `hC`
+(`List.sum_le_card_nsmul` or an induction on the gate list). -/
+theorem DAGCircuit.toNANDForm_size_le : (C.toNANDForm hC).size ≤ n + 4 * C.gates.length := by
+  have hlen := DAGCircuit.rewrite_length_le C (nandGadget n) nandGadget_correct hC
+  -- every old gate costs at most `2 + 2 = 4` new gates
+  have hsum : (C.gates.map fun g => g.args.length + 2).sum ≤ C.gates.length * 4 := by
+    have := List.sum_le_card_nsmul (C.gates.map fun g => g.args.length + 2) 4 fun y hy => by
+      obtain ⟨g, hg, rfl⟩ := List.mem_map.mp hy
+      have := hC g hg
+      omega
+    rwa [List.length_map, Nat.nsmul_eq_mul] at this
+  rw [DAGCircuit.size_eq, DAGCircuit.toNANDForm_eq]
+  omega
+
+end ToNANDForm
+
+end BoolCircuit
